# Ability

[java.lang.Object](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446) |---[ohos.app.AbilityContext](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759) |---|---ohos.aafwk.ability.Ability
    
    public class Ability
    extends [AbilityContext](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759)
    implements [ILifecycle](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ilifecycle-0000001054238677)  
  
---  
An ability is an abstraction of functionalities that an OHOS application can provide. Abilities of OHOS applications are classified into two types: Feature Ability and Particle Ability. A Feature Ability represents an ability with a UI and is designed to interact with users. A Particle Ability represents an ability without a UI and is mainly used to provide support for FAs, for example, providing computing capabilities as a background service or providing data access capabilities as a data repository. The two types of abilities offer different templates for you to implement different functionalities. At present, OHOS provides the following types of ability templates: 

**Note:** In the description below, a Page, Service, or Data ability indicates an ability using the respective template, whereas the ability alone indicates an ability using any type of templates.

                * **Page:** An ability that displays a UI. The UI is presented through [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680). You must override the [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method and use the [setMainRoute(java.lang.String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setMainRoute-java_lang_String-) and [addActionRoute(java.lang.String,java.lang.String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__addActionRoute-java_lang_String-java_lang_String-) methods to configure the entry of a Page ability. 
                * **Service:** An ability that runs in the background and has no UI. It is used to develop services that always run in the background or are connected to other abilities. When a Service ability is connected to others, a remote object is returned, and you can use the remote object to call the functionalities provided by the Service ability. 
                * **Data:** An ability that is used to operate data and has no UI. It provides methods for inserting, deleting, updating, and querying data, and opening files. You must implement these methods. 
**Ability Profile**

Each application has a configuration file: **config.json**. This file is stored in the root directory of Java code.
    
    ├── entry
     │   ├── libs
     │   ├── src
     │   │   ├── main
     │   │   │   ├── java
     │   │   │   │   └── com
     │   │   │   │       └── example
     │   │   │   │           └── myapplication
     │   │   │   │               ├── slice
     │   │   │   │               │   └── PageAbilitySlice.java
     │   │   │   │               └── PageAbility.java
     │   │   │   ├── resources
     │   │   │   │     ├── base
     │   │   │   │     │   ├── element
     │   │   │   │     │   │   └── string.json
     │   │   │   │     │   └── media
     │   │   │   │     │       └── icon.png
     │   │   │   │     └── rawfile
     │   │   │   └── config.json
     

Description of the file structure of an application is as follows: 

**entry** stores the code, resource files, and configuration file of the application. The folder name is editable.

**entry/libs** stores third-party library files. This directory is automatically generated by the IDE upon project creation.

**entry/src/main/java** is used for code development. You can change file names in this directory as required. This directory is automatically generated by the IDE upon project creation.

**entry/src/main/resources/base/media** stores PNG and JPG image files for your application. This directory is automatically generated by the IDE upon project creation.

**entry/src/main/resources/base/element** stores resource files used for reading text resources. This directory is automatically generated by the IDE upon project creation.

The **entry/src/main/config.json** file is located in the **main** root directory. This file contains configuration information about the application. The system runs the application and displays contents on the UI based on the content of this file. This file is automatically generated by the IDE upon project creation.

To develop a new ability, you must register the ability in the **config.json** file. Example code:
    
    {
         "module":{
             ...
             "abilities":[
               {
                   ...
                   "description": "Main ability of hiworld",
                   "name": ".MainAbility",
                   "label": "main ability",
                   "icon": "main-ability.png",
                   "type": "page",
                   "visible": true,
                   "orientation": "unspecified",
                   "launch-mode": "standard",
                   ...
               }
             ]
             ...
         }
     }
     

**Note:** All abilities of an application must be registered in this file and attached to the abilities tag. 

                * The type tag indicates the type of templates used by an ability, and its value page, service, or provider indicates the Page, Service, or Data template, respectively. The type tag must be specified.
                * The name tag indicates the name of an ability and must be specified.
                * You can retain the default values for other tags.
**Ability Lifecycle**

As the fundamental unit of an application, an ability has the following four lifecycle states:

                * **INITIAL:** The ability is loaded to the memory but is not running. It is the initial state of all abilities.
                * **INACTIVE:** The ability has been loaded and executed but is not interactive. Generally, it is an intermediate state before the ability changes to **ACTIVE** or **BACKGROUND**. In this state, the UI may be visible but cannot receive input events.
                * **ACTIVE:** The ability is visible and interactive. It is considered that the ability has focus.
                * **BACKGROUND:** The ability is invisible. If system memory becomes insufficient, abilities in this state are destroyed first.

The figure below shows a complete ability lifecycle. A specific callback is executed for each state transition. You can override the callback methods.

![](https://communityfile-drcn.op.dbankcloud.cn/FileServer/getFile/cmtyPub/011/111/111/0000000000011111111.20231116085831.52100450866301476722473732755413:50001231000000:2800:89144976068BC547CE66C560DCEA8ABDF2ABFBC62AABF327FEC1EC0CDECE2082.png?needInitFileName=true?needInitFileName=true)

**Note:** All Page abilities must implement [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) to set their UIs. To override a lifecycle callback method, you must first call the callback method corresponding to the parent class, for example, super.onStart(). The state transition is implemented on the main thread. Therefore, you are advised to execute short logic in the lifecycle callback to prevent the main thread of the ability from being blocked.

The following are all lifecycle callback methods of the ability:
    
    public class MainAbility extends Ability {
          protected void onStart(Intent intent);
    
          protected void onActive();
    
          protected void onInactive();
    
          protected void onForeground(Intent intent);
    
          protected void onBackground();
    
          protected void onStop();
      }

For details on lifecycle callback, see [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680).

**Starting Abilities**

The **[startAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-)** method is used to start a new ability, which is placed at the top of the ability stack. It uses the parameter **[Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019)** to describe the ability. The code below shows how to start an ability:
    
    Button button = new Button(this);
             button.setClickedListener(listener -> {
                 Operation operation = new Intent.OperationBuilder()
                         .withDeviceId("")
                         .withBundleName("ohos.hiworld")
                         .withAbilityName("ohos.hiworld.MainAbility")
                         .build();
    
                 Intent intent = new Intent();
                 intent.setOperation(operation);
                 intent.setParam("age", 10);
    
                 startAbility(intent);
             });
     

**Connecting Abilities**

The **[connectAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,ohos.aafwk.ability.IAbilityConnection)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__connectAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-ohos_aafwk_ability_IAbilityConnection-)** method is used to connect to a Service ability. It has two parameters: [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) and [IAbilityConnection](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilityconnection-0000001054678678). [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) provides information about the target Service ability to connect, and [IAbilityConnection](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilityconnection-0000001054678678) is a callback object used to notify you of the connection result (success or failure).

In contrast to [connectAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,ohos.aafwk.ability.IAbilityConnection)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__connectAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-ohos_aafwk_ability_IAbilityConnection-), the [disconnectAbility(ohos.aafwk.ability.IAbilityConnection)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__disconnectAbility-ohos_aafwk_ability_IAbilityConnection-) method is used to disconnect from a connected Service ability.

For Service abilities, you can override the [onConnect(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onConnect-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to provide a **RemoteObject** for calling. By default, null is returned.
    
    protected IRemoteObject onConnect(Intent intent) {
             return null;
         }

| [java.lang.Object](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446) |---[ohos.app.AbilityContext](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759) |---|---ohos.aafwk.ability.Ability
    
    public class Ability
    extends [AbilityContext](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759)
    implements [ILifecycle](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ilifecycle-0000001054238677)  
  
---  
An ability is an abstraction of functionalities that an OHOS application can provide. Abilities of OHOS applications are classified into two types: Feature Ability and Particle Ability. A Feature Ability represents an ability with a UI and is designed to interact with users. A Particle Ability represents an ability without a UI and is mainly used to provide support for FAs, for example, providing computing capabilities as a background service or providing data access capabilities as a data repository. The two types of abilities offer different templates for you to implement different functionalities. At present, OHOS provides the following types of ability templates: 

**Note:** In the description below, a Page, Service, or Data ability indicates an ability using the respective template, whereas the ability alone indicates an ability using any type of templates.

                * **Page:** An ability that displays a UI. The UI is presented through [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680). You must override the [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method and use the [setMainRoute(java.lang.String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setMainRoute-java_lang_String-) and [addActionRoute(java.lang.String,java.lang.String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__addActionRoute-java_lang_String-java_lang_String-) methods to configure the entry of a Page ability. 
                * **Service:** An ability that runs in the background and has no UI. It is used to develop services that always run in the background or are connected to other abilities. When a Service ability is connected to others, a remote object is returned, and you can use the remote object to call the functionalities provided by the Service ability. 
                * **Data:** An ability that is used to operate data and has no UI. It provides methods for inserting, deleting, updating, and querying data, and opening files. You must implement these methods. 
**Ability Profile**

Each application has a configuration file: **config.json**. This file is stored in the root directory of Java code.
    
    ├── entry
     │   ├── libs
     │   ├── src
     │   │   ├── main
     │   │   │   ├── java
     │   │   │   │   └── com
     │   │   │   │       └── example
     │   │   │   │           └── myapplication
     │   │   │   │               ├── slice
     │   │   │   │               │   └── PageAbilitySlice.java
     │   │   │   │               └── PageAbility.java
     │   │   │   ├── resources
     │   │   │   │     ├── base
     │   │   │   │     │   ├── element
     │   │   │   │     │   │   └── string.json
     │   │   │   │     │   └── media
     │   │   │   │     │       └── icon.png
     │   │   │   │     └── rawfile
     │   │   │   └── config.json
     

Description of the file structure of an application is as follows: 

**entry** stores the code, resource files, and configuration file of the application. The folder name is editable.

**entry/libs** stores third-party library files. This directory is automatically generated by the IDE upon project creation.

**entry/src/main/java** is used for code development. You can change file names in this directory as required. This directory is automatically generated by the IDE upon project creation.

**entry/src/main/resources/base/media** stores PNG and JPG image files for your application. This directory is automatically generated by the IDE upon project creation.

**entry/src/main/resources/base/element** stores resource files used for reading text resources. This directory is automatically generated by the IDE upon project creation.

The **entry/src/main/config.json** file is located in the **main** root directory. This file contains configuration information about the application. The system runs the application and displays contents on the UI based on the content of this file. This file is automatically generated by the IDE upon project creation.

To develop a new ability, you must register the ability in the **config.json** file. Example code:
    
    {
         "module":{
             ...
             "abilities":[
               {
                   ...
                   "description": "Main ability of hiworld",
                   "name": ".MainAbility",
                   "label": "main ability",
                   "icon": "main-ability.png",
                   "type": "page",
                   "visible": true,
                   "orientation": "unspecified",
                   "launch-mode": "standard",
                   ...
               }
             ]
             ...
         }
     }
     

**Note:** All abilities of an application must be registered in this file and attached to the abilities tag. 

                * The type tag indicates the type of templates used by an ability, and its value page, service, or provider indicates the Page, Service, or Data template, respectively. The type tag must be specified.
                * The name tag indicates the name of an ability and must be specified.
                * You can retain the default values for other tags.
**Ability Lifecycle**

As the fundamental unit of an application, an ability has the following four lifecycle states:

                * **INITIAL:** The ability is loaded to the memory but is not running. It is the initial state of all abilities.
                * **INACTIVE:** The ability has been loaded and executed but is not interactive. Generally, it is an intermediate state before the ability changes to **ACTIVE** or **BACKGROUND**. In this state, the UI may be visible but cannot receive input events.
                * **ACTIVE:** The ability is visible and interactive. It is considered that the ability has focus.
                * **BACKGROUND:** The ability is invisible. If system memory becomes insufficient, abilities in this state are destroyed first.

The figure below shows a complete ability lifecycle. A specific callback is executed for each state transition. You can override the callback methods.

![](https://communityfile-drcn.op.dbankcloud.cn/FileServer/getFile/cmtyPub/011/111/111/0000000000011111111.20231116085831.52100450866301476722473732755413:50001231000000:2800:89144976068BC547CE66C560DCEA8ABDF2ABFBC62AABF327FEC1EC0CDECE2082.png?needInitFileName=true?needInitFileName=true)

**Note:** All Page abilities must implement [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) to set their UIs. To override a lifecycle callback method, you must first call the callback method corresponding to the parent class, for example, super.onStart(). The state transition is implemented on the main thread. Therefore, you are advised to execute short logic in the lifecycle callback to prevent the main thread of the ability from being blocked.

The following are all lifecycle callback methods of the ability:
    
    public class MainAbility extends Ability {
          protected void onStart(Intent intent);
    
          protected void onActive();
    
          protected void onInactive();
    
          protected void onForeground(Intent intent);
    
          protected void onBackground();
    
          protected void onStop();
      }

For details on lifecycle callback, see [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680).

**Starting Abilities**

The **[startAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-)** method is used to start a new ability, which is placed at the top of the ability stack. It uses the parameter **[Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019)** to describe the ability. The code below shows how to start an ability:
    
    Button button = new Button(this);
             button.setClickedListener(listener -> {
                 Operation operation = new Intent.OperationBuilder()
                         .withDeviceId("")
                         .withBundleName("ohos.hiworld")
                         .withAbilityName("ohos.hiworld.MainAbility")
                         .build();
    
                 Intent intent = new Intent();
                 intent.setOperation(operation);
                 intent.setParam("age", 10);
    
                 startAbility(intent);
             });
     

**Connecting Abilities**

The **[connectAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,ohos.aafwk.ability.IAbilityConnection)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__connectAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-ohos_aafwk_ability_IAbilityConnection-)** method is used to connect to a Service ability. It has two parameters: [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) and [IAbilityConnection](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilityconnection-0000001054678678). [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) provides information about the target Service ability to connect, and [IAbilityConnection](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilityconnection-0000001054678678) is a callback object used to notify you of the connection result (success or failure).

In contrast to [connectAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,ohos.aafwk.ability.IAbilityConnection)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__connectAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-ohos_aafwk_ability_IAbilityConnection-), the [disconnectAbility(ohos.aafwk.ability.IAbilityConnection)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__disconnectAbility-ohos_aafwk_ability_IAbilityConnection-) method is used to disconnect from a connected Service ability.

For Service abilities, you can override the [onConnect(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onConnect-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to provide a **RemoteObject** for calling. By default, null is returned.
    
    protected IRemoteObject onConnect(Intent intent) {
             return null;
         }

#### Constructor Detail# Ability

[java.lang.Object](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446) |---[ohos.app.AbilityContext](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759) |---|---ohos.aafwk.ability.Ability
    
    public class Ability
    extends [AbilityContext](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759)
    implements [ILifecycle](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ilifecycle-0000001054238677)  
  
---  
An ability is an abstraction of functionalities that an OHOS application can provide. Abilities of OHOS applications are classified into two types: Feature Ability and Particle Ability. A Feature Ability represents an ability with a UI and is designed to interact with users. A Particle Ability represents an ability without a UI and is mainly used to provide support for FAs, for example, providing computing capabilities as a background service or providing data access capabilities as a data repository. The two types of abilities offer different templates for you to implement different functionalities. At present, OHOS provides the following types of ability templates: 

**Note:** In the description below, a Page, Service, or Data ability indicates an ability using the respective template, whereas the ability alone indicates an ability using any type of templates.

                * **Page:** An ability that displays a UI. The UI is presented through [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680). You must override the [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method and use the [setMainRoute(java.lang.String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setMainRoute-java_lang_String-) and [addActionRoute(java.lang.String,java.lang.String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__addActionRoute-java_lang_String-java_lang_String-) methods to configure the entry of a Page ability. 
                * **Service:** An ability that runs in the background and has no UI. It is used to develop services that always run in the background or are connected to other abilities. When a Service ability is connected to others, a remote object is returned, and you can use the remote object to call the functionalities provided by the Service ability. 
                * **Data:** An ability that is used to operate data and has no UI. It provides methods for inserting, deleting, updating, and querying data, and opening files. You must implement these methods. 
**Ability Profile**

Each application has a configuration file: **config.json**. This file is stored in the root directory of Java code.
    
    ├── entry
     │   ├── libs
     │   ├── src
     │   │   ├── main
     │   │   │   ├── java
     │   │   │   │   └── com
     │   │   │   │       └── example
     │   │   │   │           └── myapplication
     │   │   │   │               ├── slice
     │   │   │   │               │   └── PageAbilitySlice.java
     │   │   │   │               └── PageAbility.java
     │   │   │   ├── resources
     │   │   │   │     ├── base
     │   │   │   │     │   ├── element
     │   │   │   │     │   │   └── string.json
     │   │   │   │     │   └── media
     │   │   │   │     │       └── icon.png
     │   │   │   │     └── rawfile
     │   │   │   └── config.json
     

Description of the file structure of an application is as follows: 

**entry** stores the code, resource files, and configuration file of the application. The folder name is editable.

**entry/libs** stores third-party library files. This directory is automatically generated by the IDE upon project creation.

**entry/src/main/java** is used for code development. You can change file names in this directory as required. This directory is automatically generated by the IDE upon project creation.

**entry/src/main/resources/base/media** stores PNG and JPG image files for your application. This directory is automatically generated by the IDE upon project creation.

**entry/src/main/resources/base/element** stores resource files used for reading text resources. This directory is automatically generated by the IDE upon project creation.

The **entry/src/main/config.json** file is located in the **main** root directory. This file contains configuration information about the application. The system runs the application and displays contents on the UI based on the content of this file. This file is automatically generated by the IDE upon project creation.

To develop a new ability, you must register the ability in the **config.json** file. Example code:
    
    {
         "module":{
             ...
             "abilities":[
               {
                   ...
                   "description": "Main ability of hiworld",
                   "name": ".MainAbility",
                   "label": "main ability",
                   "icon": "main-ability.png",
                   "type": "page",
                   "visible": true,
                   "orientation": "unspecified",
                   "launch-mode": "standard",
                   ...
               }
             ]
             ...
         }
     }
     

**Note:** All abilities of an application must be registered in this file and attached to the abilities tag. 

                * The type tag indicates the type of templates used by an ability, and its value page, service, or provider indicates the Page, Service, or Data template, respectively. The type tag must be specified.
                * The name tag indicates the name of an ability and must be specified.
                * You can retain the default values for other tags.
**Ability Lifecycle**

As the fundamental unit of an application, an ability has the following four lifecycle states:

                * **INITIAL:** The ability is loaded to the memory but is not running. It is the initial state of all abilities.
                * **INACTIVE:** The ability has been loaded and executed but is not interactive. Generally, it is an intermediate state before the ability changes to **ACTIVE** or **BACKGROUND**. In this state, the UI may be visible but cannot receive input events.
                * **ACTIVE:** The ability is visible and interactive. It is considered that the ability has focus.
                * **BACKGROUND:** The ability is invisible. If system memory becomes insufficient, abilities in this state are destroyed first.

The figure below shows a complete ability lifecycle. A specific callback is executed for each state transition. You can override the callback methods.

![](https://communityfile-drcn.op.dbankcloud.cn/FileServer/getFile/cmtyPub/011/111/111/0000000000011111111.20231116085831.52100450866301476722473732755413:50001231000000:2800:89144976068BC547CE66C560DCEA8ABDF2ABFBC62AABF327FEC1EC0CDECE2082.png?needInitFileName=true?needInitFileName=true)

**Note:** All Page abilities must implement [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) to set their UIs. To override a lifecycle callback method, you must first call the callback method corresponding to the parent class, for example, super.onStart(). The state transition is implemented on the main thread. Therefore, you are advised to execute short logic in the lifecycle callback to prevent the main thread of the ability from being blocked.

The following are all lifecycle callback methods of the ability:
    
    public class MainAbility extends Ability {
          protected void onStart(Intent intent);
    
          protected void onActive();
    
          protected void onInactive();
    
          protected void onForeground(Intent intent);
    
          protected void onBackground();
    
          protected void onStop();
      }

For details on lifecycle callback, see [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680).

**Starting Abilities**

The **[startAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-)** method is used to start a new ability, which is placed at the top of the ability stack. It uses the parameter **[Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019)** to describe the ability. The code below shows how to start an ability:
    
    Button button = new Button(this);
             button.setClickedListener(listener -> {
                 Operation operation = new Intent.OperationBuilder()
                         .withDeviceId("")
                         .withBundleName("ohos.hiworld")
                         .withAbilityName("ohos.hiworld.MainAbility")
                         .build();
    
                 Intent intent = new Intent();
                 intent.setOperation(operation);
                 intent.setParam("age", 10);
    
                 startAbility(intent);
             });
     

**Connecting Abilities**

The **[connectAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,ohos.aafwk.ability.IAbilityConnection)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__connectAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-ohos_aafwk_ability_IAbilityConnection-)** method is used to connect to a Service ability. It has two parameters: [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) and [IAbilityConnection](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilityconnection-0000001054678678). [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) provides information about the target Service ability to connect, and [IAbilityConnection](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilityconnection-0000001054678678) is a callback object used to notify you of the connection result (success or failure).

In contrast to [connectAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,ohos.aafwk.ability.IAbilityConnection)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__connectAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-ohos_aafwk_ability_IAbilityConnection-), the [disconnectAbility(ohos.aafwk.ability.IAbilityConnection)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__disconnectAbility-ohos_aafwk_ability_IAbilityConnection-) method is used to disconnect from a connected Service ability.

For Service abilities, you can override the [onConnect(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onConnect-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to provide a **RemoteObject** for calling. By default, null is returned.
    
    protected IRemoteObject onConnect(Intent intent) {
             return null;
         }
     

**Since:**

1

**Connecting Abilities**

The **[connectAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,ohos.aafwk.ability.IAbilityConnection)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__connectAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-ohos_aafwk_ability_IAbilityConnection-)** method is used to connect to a Service ability. It has two parameters: [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) and [IAbilityConnection](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilityconnection-0000001054678678). [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) provides information about the target Service ability to connect, and [IAbilityConnection](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilityconnection-0000001054678678) is a callback object used to notify you of the connection result (success or failure).

In contrast to [connectAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,ohos.aafwk.ability.IAbilityConnection)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__connectAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-ohos_aafwk_ability_IAbilityConnection-), the [disconnectAbility(ohos.aafwk.ability.IAbilityConnection)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__disconnectAbility-ohos_aafwk_ability_IAbilityConnection-) method is used to disconnect from a connected Service ability.

For Service abilities, you can override the [onConnect(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onConnect-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to provide a **RemoteObject** for calling. By default, null is returned.
    
    protected IRemoteObject onConnect(Intent intent) {
             return null;
         }
     

**Since:**

1

#### [h2]postTask

public void postTask​([Runnable](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/runnable-0000001054838396) task, long delayTime) throws [IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724)

Posts a scheduled Runnable task to a new non-UI thread. 

The task posted via this method will be executed in a new thread, which allows you to perform certain time-consuming operations. To use this method, you must also override the [supportHighPerformanceUI()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__supportHighPerformanceUI--) method. Additionally, the usage of this method must comply with the following constraints: 

                1. This method can only be used to initialize the component tree in parallel mode.
                2. The task can only be processed during system cold start.
                3. If the parallel loading mechanism is used, the component tree-related operations to be performed in [onActive()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onActive--) and [onStop()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStop--) of the ability slice must also be added to the parallel thread.
                4. You must run [setUIContent(ohos.agp.components.ComponentContainer)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setUIContent-ohos_agp_components_ComponentContainer-) to unlock the UI thread and wait until the component tree is ready.
                5. Other time-consuming operations, such as I/O and network processing, cannot be added to the parallel task queue.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
task  |  Indicates the Runnable task to post.  
delayTime  |  Indicates the number of milliseconds after which the task will be executed.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if the input parameter is invalid.  
  
**Since:**

4

#### [h2]getLayoutParams

public [WindowManager.LayoutConfig](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/windowmanager_layoutconfig-0000001054120018) getLayoutParams​()

Obtains window layout parameters of an ability. Layout parameters must be set for windows of all abilities. These parameters are provided for the parent window to specify how to lay out the windows of the current ability. 

You can use layout parameters to control the window attributes of an ability, such as the position, length, and width.

**Returns:**

Returns window layout parameters of the ability; returns null if no layout parameter is set.

**Since:**

1

You can use the [IContinuationRegisterManager](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/icontinuationregistermanager-0000001138987467) object to interact with the hop task management service, including registering and unregistering the ability to migrate, updating the device connection state, and showing the list of devices that can be selected for ability migration. **Returns:** Returns the [IContinuationRegisterManager](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/icontinuationregistermanager-0000001138987467) object. **Since:** 5 [h2]onHotkeyTriggered public boolean onHotkeyTriggered​(int keyCode, [KeyEvent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/keyevent-0000001054120285) keyEvent) Called when a hotkey is pressed. **Parameters:** | Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
keyCode  |  Indicates the key code..  
keyEvent  |  Indicates the triggered key event.  
**Returns:** Returns true if the key event is handled in this callback; returns false otherwise. **Since:** 5 [h2]onKeyDown public boolean onKeyDown​(int keyCode, [KeyEvent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/keyevent-0000001054120285) keyEvent) Called when a key is pressed. When any component in the Ability gains focus, the key-down event for the component will be handled first. This callback will not be invoked if the callback triggered for the key-down event of the component returns true. The default implementation of this callback does nothing and returns false. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
keyCode  |  Indicates the code of the key pressed.  
keyEvent  |  Indicates the key-down event.  
**Returns:** Returns true if this event is handled and will not be passed further; returns false if this event is not handled and should be passed to other handlers. **Since:** 3 **See Also:** [onKeyUp(int, ohos.multimodalinput.event.KeyEvent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onKeyUp-int-ohos_multimodalinput_event_KeyEvent-) [h2]onKeyUp public boolean onKeyUp​(int keyCode, [KeyEvent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/keyevent-0000001054120285) keyEvent) Called when a key is released. When any component in the Ability gains focus, the key-up event for the component will be handled first. This callback will not be invoked if the callback triggered for the key-up event of the component returns true. The default implementation of this callback does nothing and returns false. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
keyCode  |  Indicates the code of the key released.  
keyEvent  |  Indicates the key-up event.  
**Returns:** Returns true if this event is handled and will not be passed further; returns false if this event is not handled and should be passed to other handlers. **Since:** 3 **See Also:** [onKeyDown(int, ohos.multimodalinput.event.KeyEvent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onKeyDown-int-ohos_multimodalinput_event_KeyEvent-) [h2]onKeyPressAndHold public boolean onKeyPressAndHold​(int keyCode, [KeyEvent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/keyevent-0000001054120285) keyEvent) Called when a key is pressed and held. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
keyCode  |  Indicates the key code.  
keyEvent  |  Indicates the triggered key event.  
**Returns:** Returns true if the key event is handled in this callback; returns false otherwise. **Since:** 5 [h2]onBackPressed protected void onBackPressed​() Called back when the Back key is pressed. The default implementation destroys the ability. You can override this method. **Since:** 3 [h2]connectAbility public boolean connectAbility​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, [IAbilityConnection](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilityconnection-0000001054678678) conn) Connects an ability to a Service ability. This method can be called by a Page or Service ability, but the destination of the connection must be a Service ability. You must implement the [IAbilityConnection](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilityconnection-0000001054678678) interface to obtain the proxy of the target Service ability when the Service ability is connected. The calling [Context](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/context-0000001054440065) must have been started and have not been destroyed. Otherwise, this method returns false. For example, when an [AbilityForm](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityform-0000001054199952) supplier calls this method in a callback triggered upon a component click or touch event, false may be returned because the supplier may not be started. **Specified by:** [connectAbility](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/context-0000001054440065#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054440065__connectAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-ohos_aafwk_ability_IAbilityConnection-) in interface [ContextUri](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/uri-0000001054559009) uri, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) mimeType, [PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) pacMap, T args, [PipeFileWriter](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pipefilewriter-0000001060039121)<T> pipeFileWriter)

Creates a data pipe, calls a background thread to write the data pointed to by the specified Uri into the pipe, and returns the FileDescriptor object used for data reading.

#### [h2]delete

public int delete​([Uri](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/uri-0000001054559009) uri, [DataAbilityPredicates](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dataabilitypredicates-0000001054200074) predicates)

Deletes one or more data records. This method should be implemented by a Data ability. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
uri  |  Indicates the database table storing the data to delete.  
predicates  |  Indicates filter criteria. If this parameter is null, all data records will be deleted by default.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns the number of data records deleted.

1

#### [h2]update

public int update​([Uri](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/uri-0000001054559009) uri, [ValuesBucket](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/valuesbucket-0000001054678818) value, [DataAbilityPredicates](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dataabilitypredicates-0000001054200074) predicates)

Updates one or more data records in the database. This method should be implemented by a Data ability. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
uri  |  Indicates the database table storing the data to update.  
value  |  Indicates the data to update. This parameter can be null.  
predicates  |  Indicates filter criteria. If this parameter is null, all data records will be updated by default.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns the number of data records updated.

**Since:**

1

protected void | [[Uri](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/uri-0000001054559009) uri, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460)[] columns, [DataAbilityPredicates](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dataabilitypredicates-0000001054200074) predicates) Queries one or more data records in the database. This method should be implemented by a Data ability. **Parameters:** | Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
uri  |  Indicates the database table storing the data to query.  
columns  |  Indicates the columns to be queried, in array, for example, {"name","age"}. You should define the processing logic when this parameter is null.  
predicates  |  Indicates filter criteria. If this parameter is null, all data records will be queried by default.  
**Returns:** Returns the queried data. **Since:** 1 [h2]executeBatch public [DataAbilityResult](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dataabilityresult-0000001054838634)[] executeBatch​([ArrayList](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/arraylist-0000001054238589)<[DataAbilityOperation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dataabilityoperation-0000001054238679)> operations) throws [OperationExecuteException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/operationexecuteexception-0000001054238683) Performs batch operations on the database. This method should be implemented by a Data ability. The batch operations to be executed on the database are encapsulated as [DataAbilityOperation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dataabilityoperation-0000001054238679) objects. Each object corresponds to an operation, for example, inserting or deleting. The call result of each operation is saved to and returned by the data array of the [DataAbilityResult](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dataabilityresult-0000001054838634) type. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
operations  |  Indicates the data operation list, which can contain multiple operations on the database.  
**Returns:** Returns the result of each operation, in array. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[OperationExecuteException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/operationexecuteexception-0000001054238683) |  Throws this exception if the batch operation fails.  
**Since:** 1 [h2]openFile public [FileDescriptor](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filedescriptor-0000001054119688) openFile​([Uri](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/uri-0000001054559009) uri, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) mode) throws [FileNotFoundException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filenotfoundexception-0000001054558439) Opens a file. This method should be implemented by a Data ability. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
uri  |  Indicates the path of the file to open.  
mode  |  Indicates the open mode, which can be "r" for read-only access, "w" for write-only access (erasing whatever data is currently in the file), "wt" for write access that truncates any existing file, "wa" for write-only access to append to any existing data, "rw" for read and write access on any existing data, or "rwt" for read and write access that truncates any existing file.  
**Returns:** Returns the [FileDescriptor](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filedescriptor-0000001054119688) object of the file descriptor. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FileNotFoundException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filenotfoundexception-0000001054558439) |  Throws this exception if the file does not exist.

void | [postTask](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__postTask-java_lang_Runnable-long-)​([Runnable](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/runnable-0000001054838396) task, long delayTime) |  Posts a scheduled Runnable task to a new non-UI thread.  ](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/constant-values-0000001054359004#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054359004__ohos_aafwk_ability_Ability_FORM_VISIBLE) Constructor Detail [h2]Ability public Ability​() Method Detail [h2]onInitialized protected void onInitialized​([AbilityInfo](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo-0000001054358782) abilityInfo) Called to carry AbilityInfo to this ability after the ability is initialized. **Parameters:** | Parameter Name | Parameter Description

abilityInfo  |  Indicates the AbilityInfo object containing information about this ability.  
**Since:** 5 [h2]postTask public void postTask​([Runnable](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/runnable-0000001054838396) task, long delayTime) throws [IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) Posts a scheduled Runnable task to a new non-UI thread. The task posted via this method will be executed in a new thread, which allows you to perform certain time-consuming operations. To use this method, you must also override the [supportHighPerformanceUI()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__supportHighPerformanceUI--) method. Additionally, the usage of this method must comply with the following constraints: 
                1. This method can only be used to initialize the component tree in parallel mode.
                2. The task can only be processed during system cold start.
                3. If the parallel loading mechanism is used, the component tree-related operations to be performed in [onActive()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onActive--) and [onStop()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStop--) of the ability slice must also be added to the parallel thread.
                4. You must run [setUIContent(ohos.agp.components.ComponentContainer)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setUIContent-ohos_agp_components_ComponentContainer-) to unlock the UI thread and wait until the component tree is ready.
                5. Other time-consuming operations, such as I/O and network processing, cannot be added to the parallel task queue.
**Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
task  |  Indicates the Runnable task to post.  
delayTime  |  Indicates the number of milliseconds after which the task will be executed.  
**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if the input parameter is invalid.  
**Since:** 4 [h2]supportHighPerformanceUI public boolean supportHighPerformanceUI​() Checks whether this ability supports high-performance UI rendering. You should override this method and make it return true if you want your ability to support high-performance UI rendering. **Returns:** Returns true if this ability supports high-performance UI rendering; returns false otherwise. **Since:** 4 [h2]getAbilityPackage public [AbilityPackage](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitypackage-0000001054518692) getAbilityPackage​() Obtains the singleton [AbilityPackage](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitypackage-0000001054518692) object to which this ability belongs. **Returns:** Returns the singleton AbilityPackage object to which this ability belongs. **Since:** 3 [h2]getWindow public [Window](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/window-0000001054358748) getWindow​() Obtains the window corresponding to the current ability. You can use a [Window](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/window-0000001054358748) instance to control attributes of a window, such as the window size, position, and padding. **Returns:** Returns the window corresponding to the current ability. **Since:** 1 [h2]setUIContent public final void setUIContent​(int layoutRes) Inflates layout resources by using the layout resource ID. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
layoutRes  |  Indicates the layout resource ID, which cannot be a negative number.  
**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[AbilitySliceRuntimeException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitysliceruntimeexception-0000001054678684) |  invalid argument  
**Since:** 3 **See Also:** [setUIContent(ComponentContainer)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setUIContent-ohos_agp_components_ComponentContainer-) [h2]setUIContent public void setUIContent​([ComponentContainer](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/componentcontainer-0000001054120043) componentContainer) Inflates UI controls by using [ComponentContainer](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/componentcontainer-0000001054120043). mode  |  Indicates the open mode, which can be "r" for read-only access, "w" for write-only access (erasing whatever data is currently in the file), "wt" for write access that truncates any existing file, "wa" for write-only access to append to any existing data, "rw" for read and write access on any existing data, or "rwt" for read and write access that truncates any existing file.  
**Returns:** Returns the [RawFileDescriptor](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/rawfiledescriptor-0000001054200156) object containing file descriptor. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FileNotFoundException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filenotfoundexception-0000001054558439) |  Throws this exception if the raw file does not exist.  
**Since:** 3 [h2]createPipeFile public <T> [FileDescriptor](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filedescriptor-0000001054119688) createPipeFile​([Uri](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/uri-0000001054559009) uri, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) mimeType, [PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) pacMap, T args, [PipeFileWriter](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pipefilewriter-0000001060039121)<T> pipeFileWriter) throws [FileNotFoundException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filenotfoundexception-0000001054558439), [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) Creates a data pipe, calls a background thread to write the data pointed to by the specified Uri into the pipe, and returns the FileDescriptor object used for data reading. [PipeFileWriter](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pipefilewriter-0000001060039121)#write(FileDescriptor, Uri, String, PacMap, T)} must have been implemented for writing data to a pipe. You should disable the FileDescriptor object returned by this method after you finish using it. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
uri  |  Indicates the source Uri object whose data will be written.  
mimeType  |  Indicates the MIME type of the data to write.  
pacMap  |  Indicates the [PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) object to be passed in this call. This parameter can be null.  
args  |  Indicates the custom parameters that specify the data shared to the client. The type of this parameter is the same as the generic type in [PipeFileWriter](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pipefilewriter-0000001060039121)}.  
pipeFileWriter  |  Indicates the instance of a class that implements [PipeFileWriter](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pipefilewriter-0000001060039121)#write(FileDescriptor, Uri, String, PacMap, T)} for pipe data writing.  
**Returns:** Returns a FileDescriptor object for pipe data reading. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if the data pipe fails to be created.  
[FileNotFoundException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filenotfoundexception-0000001054558439) |  Throws this exception if the url not found.  
**Since:** 4 [h2]getFileTypes public [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460)[] getFileTypes​([Uri](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/uri-0000001054559009) uri, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) mimeTypeFilter) Obtains the MIME type of files. This method should be implemented by a Data ability. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
uri  |  Indicates the path of the files to obtain.  
mimeTypeFilter  |  Indicates the MIME type of the files to obtain. This parameter cannot be set to null. 1\. "*/*": Obtains all types supported by a Data ability.  2\. "image/*": Obtains files whose main type is image of any subtype.  3\. "*/jpg": Obtains files whose subtype is JPG of any main type.  
**Returns:** Returns the MIME type of the matched files; returns null if there is no type that matches the Data ability. **Since:** 1 [h2]call public

#### [h2]shutdown

public void shutdown​()

Shuts down this Data ability. This method should be implemented by a Data ability. 

**Since:**

5

#### [h2]getType

public [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) getType​([Uri](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/uri-0000001054559009) uri)

Obtains the MIME type matching the data specified by the URI of the Data ability. This method should be implemented by a Data ability. 

Data abilities supports general data types, including text, HTML, and JPEG.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
uri  |  Indicates the URI of the data.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns the MIME type that matches the data specified by uri.

**Since:**

1

#### [h2]isTemporary

public boolean isTemporary​()

Checks whether this ability is a temporary ability. This method is used to identify a simulated ability used for testing. 

**Returns:**

Returns true if this ability is temporary; returns false otherwise.

**Since:**

4

#### [h2]onStart

protected void onStart​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent)

Called back when an ability is started for initialization (it can be called only once in the entire lifecycle of an ability). 

Most initialization operations should be performed by calling this method. For example, you can use the current method to call [setMainRoute(java.lang.String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setMainRoute-java_lang_String-) and [addActionRoute(java.lang.String,java.lang.String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__addActionRoute-java_lang_String-java_lang_String-) to set the main router and action router for a Page ability.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates information carried when this ability is started. **Note: For a Service ability, null is passed.**  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onPostStart

protected void onPostStart​([PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) pacMap)

Called when the ability has been started by calling [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) and [onRestoreAbilityState(ohos.utils.PacMap)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onRestoreAbilityState-ohos_utils_PacMap-). 

Generally, this method is used for system classes to perform final initialization operations after an application starts. You do not need to implement this method for your application. When overriding this method, you must call the implementation of its parent class. Otherwise, an exception will be thrown.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
pacMap  |  Indicates the data most recently provided in [onSaveAbilityState(ohos.utils.PacMap)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onSaveAbilityState-ohos_utils_PacMap-).  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]onStop

protected void onStop​()

Called back before an ability is destroyed. 

This method indicates the end of the ability lifecycle. It can be executed after the ability calls the [terminateAbility()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__terminateAbility--) method or the last ability slice of the ability calls the [AbilitySlice#terminate()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__terminate--) method. Generally, this method is used to clear resources.

**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onActive

protected void onActive​()

Called back when an ability enters the **ACTIVE** state. 

The ability in the **ACTIVE** state is in the foreground and is interactive.

**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onUnloadForm

protected void onUnloadForm​()

Called when a form is released. You can use this method to release resources. 

**Since:**

5

protected void onPostActive​()

Called when the ability is completely activated after calling [onActive()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onActive--). 

Sets the reentrance transition animation type.

Field Detail [h2]FORM_INVISIBLE public static final int FORM_INVISIBLE Indicates the event that the form becomes invisible. The form provider will receive an event notification when the [onEventNotify(java.util.Map<java.lang.Long, java.lang.Integer>)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onEventNotify-java_util_Map-) callback is invoked. **Since:** 5 **See Also:** [Constant Field Values](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/constant-values-0000001054359004#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054359004__ohos_aafwk_ability_Ability_FORM_INVISIBLE) [h2]FORM_VISIBLE public static final int FORM_VISIBLE Indicates the event that the form becomes visible. The form provider will receive an event notification when the [onEventNotify(java.util.Map<java.lang.Long, java.lang.Integer>)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onEventNotify-java_util_Map-) callback is invoked. **Since:** 5 **See Also:** [Constant Field Values](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/constant-values-0000001054359004#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054359004__ohos_aafwk_ability_Ability_FORM_VISIBLE) Constructor Detail [h2]Ability public Ability​() Method Detail [h2]onInitialized protected void onInitialized​([AbilityInfo](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo-0000001054358782) abilityInfo) Called to carry AbilityInfo to this ability after the ability is initialized. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
abilityInfo  |  Indicates the AbilityInfo object containing information about this ability.  
**Since:** 5 [h2]postTask public void postTask​([Runnable](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/runnable-0000001054838396) task, long delayTime) throws [IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) Posts a scheduled Runnable task to a new non-UI thread. The task posted via this method will be executed in a new thread, which allows you to perform certain time-consuming operations. To use this method, you must also override the [supportHighPerformanceUI()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__supportHighPerformanceUI--) method. Additionally, the usage of this method must comply with the following constraints: 
                1. This method can only be used to initialize the component tree in parallel mode.
                2. The task can only be processed during system cold start.
                3. If the parallel loading mechanism is used, the component tree-related operations to be performed in [onActive()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onActive--) and [onStop()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStop--) of the ability slice must also be added to the parallel thread.
                4. You must run [setUIContent(ohos.agp.components.ComponentContainer)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setUIContent-ohos_agp_components_ComponentContainer-) to unlock the UI thread and wait until the component tree is ready.
                5. Other time-consuming operations, such as I/O and network processing, cannot be added to the parallel task queue.
**Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
task  |  Indicates the Runnable task to post.  
delayTime  |  Indicates the number of milliseconds after which the task will be executed.  
**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if the input parameter is invalid.  
**Since:** 4 [h2]supportHighPerformanceUI public boolean supportHighPerformanceUI​() Checks whether this ability supports high-performance UI rendering. You should override this method and make it return true if you want your ability to support high-performance UI rendering. **Returns:** Returns true if this ability supports high-performance UI rendering; returns false otherwise. **Since:** 4 ](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__terminateAbility--) value, [DataAbilityPredicates](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dataabilitypredicates-0000001054200074) predicates)

**Since:** 2 [h2]getContinuationRegisterManager public [IContinuationRegisterManager](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/icontinuationregistermanager-0000001138987467) getContinuationRegisterManager​() Obtains an [IContinuationRegisterManager](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/icontinuationregistermanager-0000001138987467) object. You can use the [IContinuationRegisterManager](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/icontinuationregistermanager-0000001138987467) object to interact with the hop task management service, including registering and unregistering the ability to migrate, updating the device connection state, and showing the list of devices that can be selected for ability migration. **Returns:** Returns the [IContinuationRegisterManager](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/icontinuationregistermanager-0000001138987467) object. **Since:** 5 [h2]onHotkeyTriggered public boolean onHotkeyTriggered​(int keyCode, [KeyEvent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/keyevent-0000001054120285) keyEvent) Called when a hotkey is pressed. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
keyCode  |  Indicates the key code..  
keyEvent  |  Indicates the triggered key event.  
**Returns:** Returns true if the key event is handled in this callback; returns false otherwise. **Since:** 5 [h2]onKeyDown public boolean onKeyDown​(int keyCode, [KeyEvent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/keyevent-0000001054120285) keyEvent) Called when a key is pressed. When any component in the Ability gains focus, the key-down event for the component will be handled first. This callback will not be invoked if the callback triggered for the key-down event of the component returns true. The default implementation of this callback does nothing and returns false. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
keyCode  |  Indicates the code of the key pressed.  
keyEvent  |  Indicates the key-down event.  
**Returns:** Returns true if this event is handled and will not be passed further; returns false if this event is not handled and should be passed to other handlers. **Since:** 3 **See Also:** [onKeyUp(int, ohos.multimodalinput.event.KeyEvent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onKeyUp-int-ohos_multimodalinput_event_KeyEvent-) [h2]onKeyUp public boolean onKeyUp​(int keyCode, [KeyEvent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/keyevent-0000001054120285) keyEvent) Called when a key is released. When any component in the Ability gains focus, the key-up event for the component will be handled first. This callback will not be invoked if the callback triggered for the key-up event of the component returns true. The default implementation of this callback does nothing and returns false. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
keyCode  |  Indicates the code of the key released.  
keyEvent  |  Indicates the key-up event.  
**Returns:** Returns true if this event is handled and will not be passed further; returns false if this event is not handled and should be passed to other handlers. **Since:** 3 **See Also:** [onKeyDown(int, ohos.multimodalinput.event.KeyEvent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onKeyDown-int-ohos_multimodalinput_event_KeyEvent-) [h2]onKeyPressAndHold public boolean onKeyPressAndHold​(int keyCode, [KeyEvent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/keyevent-0000001054120285) keyEvent) Called when a key is pressed and held. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
keyCode  |  Indicates the key code.  
keyEvent  |  Indicates the triggered key event.  
**Returns:** Returns true if the key event is handled in this callback; returns false otherwise. **Since:** 5 [h2]onBackPressed protected void onBackPressed​() Called back when the Back key is pressed. The default implementation destroys the ability. You can override this method. **Since:** 3 [h2]connectAbility public boolean connectAbility​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, [IAbilityConnection](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilityconnection-0000001054678678) conn) Connects an ability to a Service ability. This method can be called by a Page or Service ability, but the destination of the connection must be a Service ability. You must implement the [IAbilityConnection](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilityconnection-0000001054678678) interface to obtain the proxy of the target Service ability when the Service ability is connected. The calling [Context](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/context-0000001054440065) must have been started and have not been destroyed. Otherwise, this method returns false. For example, when an [AbilityForm](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityform-0000001054199952) supplier calls this method in a callback triggered upon a component click or touch event, false may be returned because the supplier may not be started. **Specified by:** [h2]getAbilityPackage public [AbilityPackage](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitypackage-0000001054518692) getAbilityPackage​() Obtains the singleton [AbilityPackage](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitypackage-0000001054518692) object to which this ability belongs. **Returns:** Returns the singleton AbilityPackage object to which this ability belongs. **Since:** 3 [h2]getWindow public [Window](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/window-0000001054358748) getWindow​() Obtains the window corresponding to the current ability.
