#### [h2]postTask

public void postTask​([Runnable](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/runnable-0000001054838396) task, long delayTime) throws [IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724)

Posts a scheduled Runnable task to a new non-UI thread. 

The task posted via this method will be executed in a new thread, which allows you to perform certain time-consuming operations. To use this method, you must also override the [supportHighPerformanceUI()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__supportHighPerformanceUI--) method. Additionally, the usage of this method must comply with the following constraints: 

                1. This method can only be used to initialize the component tree in parallel mode.
                2. The task can only be processed during system cold start.
                3. If the parallel loading mechanism is used, the component tree-related operations to be performed in [onActive()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onActive--) and [onStop()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStop--) of the ability slice must also be added to the parallel thread.
                4. You must run [setUIContent(ohos.agp.components.ComponentContainer)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setUIContent-ohos_agp_components_ComponentContainer-) to unlock the UI thread and wait until the component tree is ready.
                5. Other time-consuming operations, such as I/O and network processing, cannot be added to the parallel task queue.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
task  |  Indicates the Runnable task to post.  
delayTime  |  Indicates the number of milliseconds after which the task will be executed.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if the input parameter is invalid.  
  
**Since:**

4

#### [h2]onKeyDown

public boolean onKeyDown​(int keyCode, [KeyEvent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/keyevent-0000001054120285) keyEvent)

Called when a key is pressed. When any component in the Ability gains focus, the key-down event for the component will be handled first. This callback will not be invoked if the callback triggered for the key-down event of the component returns true. The default implementation of this callback does nothing and returns false. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
keyCode  |  Indicates the code of the key pressed.  
keyEvent  |  Indicates the key-down event.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns true if this event is handled and will not be passed further; returns false if this event is not handled and should be passed to other handlers.

**Since:**

1

#### [h2]executeBatch

public [DataAbilityResult](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dataabilityresult-0000001054838634)[] executeBatch​([ArrayList](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/arraylist-0000001054238589)<[DataAbilityOperation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dataabilityoperation-0000001054238679)> operations) throws [OperationExecuteException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/operationexecuteexception-0000001054238683)

Performs batch operations on the database. This method should be implemented by a Data ability. 

The batch operations to be executed on the database are encapsulated as [DataAbilityOperation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dataabilityoperation-0000001054238679) objects. Each object corresponds to an operation, for example, inserting or deleting. The call result of each operation is saved to and returned by the data array of the [DataAbilityResult](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dataabilityresult-0000001054838634) type.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
operations  |  Indicates the data operation list, which can contain multiple operations on the database.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns the result of each operation, in array.

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[OperationExecuteException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/operationexecuteexception-0000001054238683) |  Throws this exception if the batch operation fails.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]connectAbility

public boolean connectAbility​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, [IAbilityConnection](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilityconnection-0000001054678678) conn)

Connects an ability to a Service ability. 

This method can be called by a Page or Service ability, but the destination of the connection must be a Service ability. You must implement the [IAbilityConnection](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilityconnection-0000001054678678) interface to obtain the proxy of the target Service ability when the Service ability is connected.

The calling [Context](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/context-0000001054440065) must have been started and have not been destroyed. Otherwise, this method returns false. For example, when an [AbilityForm](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityform-0000001054199952) supplier calls this method in a callback triggered upon a component click or touch event, false may be returned because the supplier may not be started.

**Specified by:**

[connectAbility](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/context-0000001054440065#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054440065__connectAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-ohos_aafwk_ability_IAbilityConnection-) in interface [Context](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/context-0000001054440065)

**Overrides:**

[connectAbility](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054358759__connectAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-ohos_aafwk_ability_IAbilityConnection-) in class [AbilityContext](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759)

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the Service ability to connect.  
conn  |  Indicates the callback object when the Service ability is connected.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns true if the connection is successful; returns false otherwise.

**Generally, this method is used for system classes to perform final settings after an application is activated. You do not need to implement this method for your application. When overriding this method, you must call the implementation of its parent class. Otherwise, an exception will be thrown.

**Since:**

3

#### [h2]onInactive

protected void onInactive​()

Called back when an ability enters the **INACTIVE** state (an ability in this state is not interactive and may change to the **BACKGROUND** or **ACTIVE** state). 

**INACTIVE** is an intermediate state and does not last for a long period of time. Frequent switching between **ACTIVE** and **INACTIVE** is possible. Therefore, you can use this method to perform certain simple operations, for example, committing any changes.

**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onForeground

protected void onForeground​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent)

Called back when the state of an ability changes from **BACKGROUND** to **INACTIVE**. 

This is an intermediate state callback method. Generally, the system calls this method to refresh data status and then calls back the [onActive()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onActive--) method.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates information carried when the ability returns to the **INACTIVE** state.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onBackground

protected void onBackground​()

Called back when an ability enters the **BACKGROUND** state. 

A Page ability in the **BACKGROUND** state is invisible. You are advised to suspend threads related to the ability and clear some resources to free up system memory.

**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onCommand

[@Deprecated](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/deprecated-0000001054838412) protected void onCommand​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, boolean restart)

Deprecated. This change is effective since API version 3. Replaced by [onCommand(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,boolean,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onCommand-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-boolean-int-).

Called back when a Service ability is started. 

This method can be called only by Service abilities. You can use the [startAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to start a Service ability. Then the system calls back the current method to use the transferred intent parameter to execute its own logic.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the intent of the Service ability to start.  
restart  |  Indicates the startup mode. The value true indicates that the Service ability is restarted after being destroyed, and the value false indicates a normal startup.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onCommand

protected void onCommand​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, boolean restart, int startId)

Called back when Service is started. 

This method can be called only by Service. You can use the [startAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to start Service. Then the system calls back the current method to use the transferred intent parameter to execute its own logic.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the intent of Service to start.  
restart  |  Indicates the startup mode. The value true indicates that Service is restarted after being destroyed, and the value false indicates a normal startup.  
startId  |  Indicates the number of times the Service ability has been started. The startId is incremented by 1 every time the ability is started. For example, if the ability has been started for six times, the value of startId is 6.  
  
**Since:**

3

---|---  
  
Parameter Name

Parameter Description  
uri  |  Indicates the path of the raw file to open.  
mode  |  Indicates the open mode, which can be "r" for read-only access, "w" for write-only access (erasing whatever data is currently in the file), "wt" for write access that truncates any existing file, "wa" for write-only access to append to any existing data, "rw" for read and write access on any existing data, or "rwt" for read and write access that truncates any existing file.

#### [h2]createPipeFile

public <T> [FileDescriptor](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filedescriptor-0000001054119688) createPipeFile​([Uri](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/uri-0000001054559009) uri, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) mimeType, [PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) pacMap, T args, [PipeFileWriter](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pipefilewriter-0000001060039121)<T> pipeFileWriter) throws [FileNotFoundException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filenotfoundexception-0000001054558439), [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Creates a data pipe, calls a background thread to write the data pointed to by the specified Uri into the pipe, and returns the FileDescriptor object used for data reading. 

[PipeFileWriter](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pipefilewriter-0000001060039121)#write(FileDescriptor, Uri, String, PacMap, T)} must have been implemented for writing data to a pipe. You should disable the FileDescriptor object returned by this method after you finish using it.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
uri  |  Indicates the source Uri object whose data will be written.  
mimeType  |  Indicates the MIME type of the data to write.  
pacMap  |  Indicates the [PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) object to be passed in this call. This parameter can be null.  
args  |  Indicates the custom parameters that specify the data shared to the client. The type of this parameter is the same as the generic type in [PipeFileWriter](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pipefilewriter-0000001060039121)}.  
pipeFileWriter  |  Indicates the instance of a class that implements [PipeFileWriter](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pipefilewriter-0000001060039121)#write(FileDescriptor, Uri, String, PacMap, T)} for pipe data writing.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns a FileDescriptor object for pipe data reading.

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if the data pipe fails to be created.  
[FileNotFoundException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filenotfoundexception-0000001054558439) |  Throws this exception if the url not found.  
  
**Since:**

4

"image/*": Obtains files whose main type is image of any subtype.  3\. "*/jpg": Obtains files whose subtype is JPG of any main type.

#### [h2]onMemoryLevel

public void onMemoryLevel​(int level)

Called when the system has determined to trim the memory, for example, when the ability is running in the background and there is no enough memory for running as many background processes as possible. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
level  |  Indicates the memory trim level, which shows the current memory usage status. The values and their respective meanings are as follows: 
                * 5: The application is running properly and will not be killed, but the device is running slightly low on memory. The system may start to kill processes based on the Least Recently Used (LRU) list. 
                * 10: The application is running properly and will not be killed, but the device is running extremely low on memory. Your application should release some unnecessary resources to improve system performance, which accordingly improves the application performance. 
                * 15: The application is running properly, but the system has killed most of the cached processes based on the LRU list. In this case, your application should release as many unnecessary resources as possible. Otherwise, the system may continue to kill all the cached processes and start to kill some processes that should be kept running, for example, services running in the background. 
                * 20: The application UI is hidden, for example, because the user touched the Home key on the device. Your application should releases resources allocated for UI display to free up memory. 
                * 40: The device is running low on memory and your application is around the top of the LRU list. The system is not likely to kill your application. To keep your application processes in the cache for a longer time, your application should increase available system memory by releasing some resources that can be easily restored. In this way, your application is able to smoothly return to the foreground, instead of being restarted. 
                * 60: The device is running extremely low on memory and your application is around the middle of the LRU list. Your application may be killed later if no more memory is freed up. 
                * 80: The device is running extremely low on memory and your application is at the end of the LRU list. The system is most likely to kill your application first. In this case, your application should release all resources that can be released.   
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]onAbilityResult

protected void onAbilityResult​(int requestCode, int resultCode, [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) resultData)

Called when [startAbilityForResult(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbilityForResult-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-int-) is called to start an ability and the result is returned. 

This method is called only on Page abilities. You can start a new ability to perform some calculations and use [setResult(int,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setResult-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) to return the calculation result. Then the system calls back the current method to use the returned data to execute its own logic.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
requestCode  |  Indicates the request code returned after the ability is started. You can define the request code to identify the results returned by abilities. The value ranges from 0 to 65535.  
resultCode  |  Indicates the result code returned after the ability is started. You can define the result code to identify an error.  
resultData  |  Indicates the data returned after the ability is started. You can define the data returned. The value can be null.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]getLayoutParams

public [WindowManager.LayoutConfig](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/windowmanager_layoutconfig-0000001054120018) getLayoutParams​()

Obtains window layout parameters of an ability. Layout parameters must be set for windows of all abilities. These parameters are provided for the parent window to specify how to lay out the windows of the current ability. 

You can use layout parameters to control the window attributes of an ability, such as the position, length, and width.

**Returns:**

Returns window layout parameters of the ability; returns null if no layout parameter is set.

**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onKeyDown

public boolean onKeyDown​(int keyCode, [KeyEvent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/keyevent-0000001054120285) keyEvent)

Called when a key is pressed. When any component in the Ability gains focus, the key-down event for the component will be handled first. This callback will not be invoked if the callback triggered for the key-down event of the component returns true. The default implementation of this callback does nothing and returns false. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
keyCode  |  Indicates the code of the key pressed.  
keyEvent  |  Indicates the key-down event.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns true if this event is handled and will not be passed further; returns false if this event is not handled and should be passed to other handlers.

**Since:**

3

**See Also:**

[onKeyUp(int, ohos.multimodalinput.event.KeyEvent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onKeyUp-int-ohos_multimodalinput_event_KeyEvent-)

#### [h2]onKeyUp

public boolean onKeyUp​(int keyCode, [KeyEvent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/keyevent-0000001054120285) keyEvent)

Called when a key is released. When any component in the Ability gains focus, the key-up event for the component will be handled first. This callback will not be invoked if the callback triggered for the key-up event of the component returns true. The default implementation of this callback does nothing and returns false. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
keyCode  |  Indicates the code of the key released.  
keyEvent  |  Indicates the key-up event.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns true if this event is handled and will not be passed further; returns false if this event is not handled and should be passed to other handlers.

**Since:**

3

**See Also:**

[onKeyDown(int, ohos.multimodalinput.event.KeyEvent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onKeyDown-int-ohos_multimodalinput_event_KeyEvent-)

#### [h2]onStart

protected void onStart​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent)

Called back when an ability is started for initialization (it can be called only once in the entire lifecycle of an ability). 

Most initialization operations should be performed by calling this method. For example, you can use the current method to call [setMainRoute(java.lang.String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setMainRoute-java_lang_String-) and [addActionRoute(java.lang.String,java.lang.String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__addActionRoute-java_lang_String-java_lang_String-) to set the main router and action router for a Page ability.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates information carried when this ability is started. **Note: For a Service ability, null is passed.**  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onPostStart

protected void onPostStart​([PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) pacMap)

Called when the ability has been started by calling [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) and [onRestoreAbilityState(ohos.utils.PacMap)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onRestoreAbilityState-ohos_utils_PacMap-). 

Generally, this method is used for system classes to perform final initialization operations after an application starts. You do not need to implement this method for your application. When overriding this method, you must call the implementation of its parent class. Otherwise, an exception will be thrown.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
pacMap  |  Indicates the data most recently provided in [onSaveAbilityState(ohos.utils.PacMap)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onSaveAbilityState-ohos_utils_PacMap-).  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]onStop

protected void onStop​()

Called back before an ability is destroyed. 

This method indicates the end of the ability lifecycle. It can be executed after the ability calls the [terminateAbility()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__terminateAbility--) method or the last ability slice of the ability calls the [AbilitySlice#terminate()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__terminate--) method. Generally, this method is used to clear resources.

**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onPostActive

protected void onPostActive​()

Called when the ability is completely activated after calling [onActive()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onActive--). 

Generally, this method is used for system classes to perform final settings after an application is activated. You do not need to implement this method for your application. When overriding this method, you must call the implementation of its parent class. Otherwise, an exception will be thrown.

**Since:**

3

#### [h2]onInactive

protected void onInactive​()

Called back when an ability enters the **INACTIVE** state (an ability in this state is not interactive and may change to the **BACKGROUND** or **ACTIVE** state). 

**INACTIVE** is an intermediate state and does not last for a long period of time. Frequent switching between **ACTIVE** and **INACTIVE** is possible. Therefore, you can use this method to perform certain simple operations, for example, committing any changes.

**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onForeground

protected void onForeground​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent)

Called back when the state of an ability changes from **BACKGROUND** to **INACTIVE**. 

This is an intermediate state callback method. Generally, the system calls this method to refresh data status and then calls back the [onActive()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onActive--) method.

**Parameters:**

---|---  
uri  |  Indicates the database table storing the data to delete.  
predicates  |  Indicates filter criteria. If this parameter is null, all data records will be deleted by default.

Returns the number of data records deleted.

#### [h2]query

public [ResultSet](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/resultset-0000001054838776) query​([Uri](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/uri-0000001054559009) uri, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460)[] columns, [DataAbilityPredicates](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dataabilitypredicates-0000001054200074) predicates)

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
uri  |  Indicates the database table storing the data to query.  
columns  |  Indicates the columns to be queried, in array, for example, {"name","age"}. You should define the processing logic when this parameter is null.

#### [h2]executeBatch

public [DataAbilityResult](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dataabilityresult-0000001054838634)[] executeBatch​([ArrayList](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/arraylist-0000001054238589)<[DataAbilityOperation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dataabilityoperation-0000001054238679)> operations) throws [OperationExecuteException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/operationexecuteexception-0000001054238683)

Performs batch operations on the database. This method should be implemented by a Data ability. 

The batch operations to be executed on the database are encapsulated as [DataAbilityOperation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dataabilityoperation-0000001054238679) objects. Each object corresponds to an operation, for example, inserting or deleting. The call result of each operation is saved to and returned by the data array of the [DataAbilityResult](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dataabilityresult-0000001054838634) type.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
operations  |  Indicates the data operation list, which can contain multiple operations on the database.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns the result of each operation, in array.

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[OperationExecuteException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/operationexecuteexception-0000001054238683) |  Throws this exception if the batch operation fails.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]connectAbility

public boolean connectAbility​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, [IAbilityConnection](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilityconnection-0000001054678678) conn)

Connects an ability to a Service ability. 

This method can be called by a Page or Service ability, but the destination of the connection must be a Service ability. You must implement the [IAbilityConnection](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilityconnection-0000001054678678) interface to obtain the proxy of the target Service ability when the Service ability is connected.

The calling [Context](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/context-0000001054440065) must have been started and have not been destroyed. Otherwise, this method returns false. For example, when an [AbilityForm](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityform-0000001054199952) supplier calls this method in a callback triggered upon a component click or touch event, false may be returned because the supplier may not be started.

**Specified by:**

[connectAbility](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/context-0000001054440065#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054440065__connectAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-ohos_aafwk_ability_IAbilityConnection-) in interface [Context](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/context-0000001054440065)

**Overrides:**

[connectAbility](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054358759__connectAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-ohos_aafwk_ability_IAbilityConnection-) in class [AbilityContext](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759)

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the Service ability to connect.  
conn  |  Indicates the callback object when the Service ability is connected.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns true if the connection is successful; returns false otherwise.

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if the ability is not initialized.  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if the input parameter is null.  
  
**Since:**

1

**See Also:**

[Context.connectAbility(Intent, IAbilityConnection)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/context-0000001054440065#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054440065__connectAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-ohos_aafwk_ability_IAbilityConnection-)

#### [h2]onEventNotify

protected void onEventNotify​([Map](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/map-0000001054199858)<[Long](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/long-0000001054439731),[Integer](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/integer-0000001054119702)> formEvents)

Called when the form provider receives form events from the system. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
formEvents  |  Indicates the form events occurred. The key in the Map object indicates the form ID, and the value indicates the event type, which can be either [FORM_VISIBLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__FORM_VISIBLE) or [FORM_INVISIBLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__FORM_INVISIBLE). [FORM_VISIBLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__FORM_VISIBLE) means that the form becomes visible, and [FORM_INVISIBLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__FORM_INVISIBLE) means that the form becomes invisible.  
  
**Since:**

5

#### [h2]onTriggerFormEvent

[@Deprecated](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/deprecated-0000001054838412) protected void onTriggerFormEvent​(int formId, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) message)

Deprecated. This change is effective since API version 5. Replaced by [onTriggerFormEvent(long,java.lang.String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onTriggerFormEvent-long-java_lang_String-).

Called when a specified message event defined by the form provider is triggered. This method is valid only for JS forms. 

You must override this method to define the work to do after the message event is triggered if a message event is configured. You can define a message event for a component on a JS form. When the user touches the particular component on the form, your application, as the form provider, will be notified and receive the value of the params field contained in the event.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
formId  |  Indicates the ID of the form on which the message event is triggered, which is provided by the client to the form provider.  
message  |  Indicates the value of the params field of the message event. This parameter is used to identify the specific component on which the event is triggered.  
  
**Since:**

5

#### [h2]executeBatch

public [DataAbilityResult](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dataabilityresult-0000001054838634)[] executeBatch​([ArrayList](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/arraylist-0000001054238589)<[DataAbilityOperation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dataabilityoperation-0000001054238679)> operations) throws [OperationExecuteException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/operationexecuteexception-0000001054238683)

Performs batch operations on the database. This method should be implemented by a Data ability. 

The batch operations to be executed on the database are encapsulated as [DataAbilityOperation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dataabilityoperation-0000001054238679) objects. Each object corresponds to an operation, for example, inserting or deleting. The call result of each operation is saved to and returned by the data array of the [DataAbilityResult](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dataabilityresult-0000001054838634) type.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
operations  |  Indicates the data operation list, which can contain multiple operations on the database.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns the result of each operation, in array.

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[OperationExecuteException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/operationexecuteexception-0000001054238683) |  Throws this exception if the batch operation fails.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]openFile

public [FileDescriptor](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filedescriptor-0000001054119688) openFile​([Uri](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/uri-0000001054559009) uri, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) mode) throws [FileNotFoundException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filenotfoundexception-0000001054558439)

Opens a file. This method should be implemented by a Data ability. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
uri  |  Indicates the path of the file to open.  
mode  |  Indicates the open mode, which can be "r" for read-only access, "w" for write-only access (erasing whatever data is currently in the file), "wt" for write access that truncates any existing file, "wa" for write-only access to append to any existing data, "rw" for read and write access on any existing data, or "rwt" for read and write access that truncates any existing file.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns the [FileDescriptor](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filedescriptor-0000001054119688) object of the file descriptor.

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FileNotFoundException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filenotfoundexception-0000001054558439) |  Throws this exception if the file does not exist.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]openRawFile

public [RawFileDescriptor](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/rawfiledescriptor-0000001054200156) openRawFile​([Uri](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/uri-0000001054559009) uri, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) mode) throws [FileNotFoundException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filenotfoundexception-0000001054558439)

Opens a raw file. This method should be implemented by a Data ability. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
uri  |  Indicates the path of the raw file to open.  
mode  |  Indicates the open mode, which can be "r" for read-only access, "w" for write-only access (erasing whatever data is currently in the file), "wt" for write access that truncates any existing file, "wa" for write-only access to append to any existing data, "rw" for read and write access on any existing data, or "rwt" for read and write access that truncates any existing file.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns the [RawFileDescriptor](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/rawfiledescriptor-0000001054200156) object containing file descriptor.

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FileNotFoundException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filenotfoundexception-0000001054558439) |  Throws this exception if the raw file does not exist.  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]createPipeFile

public <T> [FileDescriptor](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filedescriptor-0000001054119688) createPipeFile​([Uri](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/uri-0000001054559009) uri, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) mimeType, [PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) pacMap, T args, [PipeFileWriter](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pipefilewriter-0000001060039121)<T> pipeFileWriter) throws [FileNotFoundException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filenotfoundexception-0000001054558439), [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Creates a data pipe, calls a background thread to write the data pointed to by the specified Uri into the pipe, and returns the FileDescriptor object used for data reading. 

[PipeFileWriter](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pipefilewriter-0000001060039121)#write(FileDescriptor, Uri, String, PacMap, T)} must have been implemented for writing data to a pipe. You should disable the FileDescriptor object returned by this method after you finish using it.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
uri  |  Indicates the source Uri object whose data will be written.  
mimeType  |  Indicates the MIME type of the data to write.  
pacMap  |  Indicates the [PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) object to be passed in this call. This parameter can be null.  
args  |  Indicates the custom parameters that specify the data shared to the client. The type of this parameter is the same as the generic type in [PipeFileWriter](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pipefilewriter-0000001060039121)}.  
pipeFileWriter  |  Indicates the instance of a class that implements [PipeFileWriter](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pipefilewriter-0000001060039121)#write(FileDescriptor, Uri, String, PacMap, T)} for pipe data writing.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns a FileDescriptor object for pipe data reading.

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if the data pipe fails to be created.  
[FileNotFoundException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filenotfoundexception-0000001054558439) |  Throws this exception if the url not found.  
  
**Since:**

4

#### [h2]onCommand

[@Deprecated](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/deprecated-0000001054838412) protected void onCommand​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, boolean restart)

Deprecated. This change is effective since API version 3. Replaced by [onCommand(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,boolean,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onCommand-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-boolean-int-).

Called back when a Service ability is started. 

This method can be called only by Service abilities. You can use the [startAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to start a Service ability. Then the system calls back the current method to use the transferred intent parameter to execute its own logic.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the intent of the Service ability to start.  
restart  |  Indicates the startup mode. The value true indicates that the Service ability is restarted after being destroyed, and the value false indicates a normal startup.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onCommand

protected void onCommand​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, boolean restart, int startId)

Called back when Service is started. 

This method can be called only by Service. You can use the [startAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to start Service. Then the system calls back the current method to use the transferred intent parameter to execute its own logic.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the intent of Service to start.  
restart  |  Indicates the startup mode. The value true indicates that Service is restarted after being destroyed, and the value false indicates a normal startup.  
startId  |  Indicates the number of times the Service ability has been started. The startId is incremented by 1 every time the ability is started. For example, if the ability has been started for six times, the value of startId is 6.  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]onTouchEvent

protected boolean onTouchEvent​([TouchEvent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/touchevent-0000001054558959) event)

Called when a touch event is dispatched to this ability. The default implementation of this callback does nothing and returns false. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|--- message)  
  
Called when a specified message event defined by the form provider is triggered. This method is valid only for JS forms. 

You must override this method to define the work to do after the message event is triggered if a message event is configured. You can define a message event for a component on a JS form. When the user touches the particular component on the form, your application, as the form provider, will be notified and receive the value of the params field contained in the event.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
formId  |  Indicates the ID of the form on which the message event is triggered, which is provided by the client to the form provider.  
message  |  Indicates the value of the params field of the message event. This parameter is used to identify the specific component on which the event is triggered.  
  
**Since:**

5

#### [h2]updateForm

[@Deprecated](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/deprecated-0000001054838412) public boolean updateForm​(int formId, [ComponentProvider](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/componentprovider-0000001054440001) component) throws [FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856)

Deprecated. This change is effective since API version 5. Replaced by [updateForm(long,ohos.agp.components.ComponentProvider)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__updateForm-long-ohos_agp_components_ComponentProvider-).

Updates the content of a specified Java form. 

This method is called by a form provider to update Java form data as needed.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
formId  |  Indicates the ID of the Java form to update.  
component  |  Indicates the ComponentProvider object used to update the Java form displayed on the client.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns true if the update is successful; returns false otherwise.

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if the form fails to be obtained due to any of the following reasons: 
                * The passed formID or formBindingData is invalid. The value of formID must be larger than 0, and formBindingData must not be null.
                * An error occurred when connecting to the Form Manager Service.
                * The specified form ID does not exist. 
                * The form has been obtained by another application and cannot be updated by the current application.
                * The form is being restored.  
  
**Since:**

5

For example, you can use the current method to call [setMainRoute(java.lang.String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setMainRoute-java_lang_String-) and [addActionRoute(java.lang.String,java.lang.String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__addActionRoute-java_lang_String-java_lang_String-) to set the main router and action router for a Page ability.

---|---  
intent  |  Indicates information carried when this ability is started. **Note: For a Service ability, null is passed.**

#### [h2]onPostStart

protected void onPostStart​([PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) pacMap)

Called when the ability has been started by calling [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) and [onRestoreAbilityState(ohos.utils.PacMap)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onRestoreAbilityState-ohos_utils_PacMap-). 

Generally, this method is used for system classes to perform final initialization operations after an application starts. You do not need to implement this method for your application. When overriding this method, you must call the implementation of its parent class. Otherwise, an exception will be thrown.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
pacMap  |  Indicates the data most recently provided in [onSaveAbilityState(ohos.utils.PacMap)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onSaveAbilityState-ohos_utils_PacMap-).  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]onPostActive

protected void onPostActive​()

Called when the ability is completely activated after calling [onActive()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onActive--). 

Generally, this method is used for system classes to perform final settings after an application is activated. You do not need to implement this method for your application. When overriding this method, you must call the implementation of its parent class. Otherwise, an exception will be thrown.

**Since:**

3

#### [h2]onInactive

protected void onInactive​()

Called back when an ability enters the **INACTIVE** state (an ability in this state is not interactive and may change to the **BACKGROUND** or **ACTIVE** state). 

**INACTIVE** is an intermediate state and does not last for a long period of time. Frequent switching between **ACTIVE** and **INACTIVE** is possible. Therefore, you can use this method to perform certain simple operations, for example, committing any changes.

**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onForeground

protected void onForeground​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent)

Called back when the state of an ability changes from **BACKGROUND** to **INACTIVE**. 

This is an intermediate state callback method. Generally, the system calls this method to refresh data status and then calls back the [onActive()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onActive--) method.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates information carried when the ability returns to the **INACTIVE** state.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onBackground

protected void onBackground​()

Called back when an ability enters the **BACKGROUND** state. 

A Page ability in the **BACKGROUND** state is invisible. You are advised to suspend threads related to the ability and clear some resources to free up system memory.

**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onCommand

[@Deprecated](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/deprecated-0000001054838412) protected void onCommand​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, boolean restart)

Deprecated. This change is effective since API version 3. Replaced by [onCommand(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,boolean,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onCommand-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-boolean-int-).

Called back when a Service ability is started. 

This method can be called only by Service abilities. You can use the [startAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to start a Service ability. Then the system calls back the current method to use the transferred intent parameter to execute its own logic.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description | Throw Description  
---|---|---  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if the form fails to be obtained due to any of the following reasons: 
                * The passed formID or formBindingData is invalid. The value of formID must be larger than 0, and formBindingData must not be null.
                * An error occurred when connecting to the Form Manager Service.
                * The specified form ID does not exist. 
                * The form has been obtained by another application and cannot be updated by the current application.
                * The form is being restored.  
  
**Since:**

5

#### [h2]updateForm

[@Deprecated](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/deprecated-0000001054838412) public boolean updateForm​(int formId, [FormBindingData](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formbindingdata-0000001070961495) formBindingData) throws [FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856)

Deprecated. This change is effective since API version 5. Replaced by [updateForm(long,ohos.aafwk.ability.FormBindingData)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__updateForm-long-ohos_aafwk_ability_FormBindingData-).

Updates the content of a specified JS form. 

This method is called by a form provider to update JS form data as needed.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
formId  |  Indicates the ID of the JS form to update.  
formBindingData  |  Indicates the FormBindingData object used to update the JS form displayed on the client.

Returns true if the update is successful; returns false otherwise.

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if the form fails to be obtained due to any of the following reasons: 
                * The passed formID or component is invalid. The value of formID must be larger than 0, and component must not be null.
                * An error occurred when connecting to the Form Manager Service.
                * The specified form ID does not exist. 
                * The form has been obtained by another application and cannot be updated by the current application.
                * The form is being restored.  
  
**Since:**

5

#### [h2]setFormNextRefreshTime

public boolean setFormNextRefreshTime​(long formId, long nextTime) throws [FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856)

Sets the next dynamic refresh time of a form. 

This method is provided for form providers.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
formId  |  Indicates the ID of the form for which you want to set the refresh time.  
nextTime  |  Indicates the interval between the current time and the next refresh time in seconds. The smallest value is 300.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns true if the setting is successful; returns false otherwise.

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if any of the cases occur: 
                * The value of formId is less than or equal to 0, or the value of nextTime is less than 300.
                * An error occurred when connecting to the Form Manager Service.
                * The specified form ID does not exist.
                * The form has been obtained by another application and cannot be updated by the current application.
                * The form is being restored.
                * The number of refresh times has reached the limit.  
  
**Since:**

6

The description covers the bundle name, ability name, module name, form name, and form dimensions, which can be obtained through [ElementName#getBundleName()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/elementname-0000001054200068#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054200068__getBundleName--), [ElementName#getAbilityName()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/elementname-0000001054200068#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054200068__getAbilityName--), [AbilitySlice#PARAM_MODULE_NAME_KEY](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__PARAM_MODULE_NAME_KEY), [AbilitySlice#PARAM_FORM_NAME_KEY](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__PARAM_FORM_NAME_KEY), and [AbilitySlice#PARAM_FORM_DIMENSION_KEY](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__PARAM_FORM_DIMENSION_KEY) respectively.  
  
**Returns:**

#### [h2]onOrientationChanged

protected void onOrientationChanged​([AbilityInfo.DisplayOrientation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810) displayOrientation)

Called back when the display orientation of a screen changes. 

You can override this method to implement your logic when the display orientation changes. By default, no operation is performed when the display orientation changes.

---|---

The following values are supported: [AbilityInfo.DisplayOrientation#LANDSCAPE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054518810__LANDSCAPE), [AbilityInfo.DisplayOrientation#PORTRAIT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054518810__PORTRAIT), [AbilityInfo.DisplayOrientation#FOLLOWRECENT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054518810__FOLLOWRECENT), and [AbilityInfo.DisplayOrientation#UNSPECIFIED](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054518810__UNSPECIFIED).  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]onMemoryLevel

public void onMemoryLevel​(int level)

Called when the system has determined to trim the memory, for example, when the ability is running in the background and there is no enough memory for running as many background processes as possible. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
level  |  Indicates the memory trim level, which shows the current memory usage status. The values and their respective meanings are as follows: 
                * 5: The application is running properly and will not be killed, but the device is running slightly low on memory. The system may start to kill processes based on the Least Recently Used (LRU) list. 
                * 10: The application is running properly and will not be killed, but the device is running extremely low on memory. Your application should release some unnecessary resources to improve system performance, which accordingly improves the application performance. 
                * 15: The application is running properly, but the system has killed most of the cached processes based on the LRU list. In this case, your application should release as many unnecessary resources as possible. Otherwise, the system may continue to kill all the cached processes and start to kill some processes that should be kept running, for example, services running in the background. 
                * 20: The application UI is hidden, for example, because the user touched the Home key on the device. Your application should releases resources allocated for UI display to free up memory. 
                * 40: The device is running low on memory and your application is around the top of the LRU list. The system is not likely to kill your application. To keep your application processes in the cache for a longer time, your application should increase available system memory by releasing some resources that can be easily restored. In this way, your application is able to smoothly return to the foreground, instead of being restarted. 
                * 60: The device is running extremely low on memory and your application is around the middle of the LRU list. Your application may be killed later if no more memory is freed up. 
                * 80: The device is running extremely low on memory and your application is at the end of the LRU list. The system is most likely to kill your application first. In this case, your application should release all resources that can be released.   
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]onReconnect

public void onReconnect​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent)

Called when a new client attempts to connect to a Service ability after all previous client connections to it are disconnected. 

The Service ability must have been started but not been destroyed, that is, [startAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) has been called but [stopAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__stopAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) has not.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the intent of the Service ability being connected.  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]onAbilityResult

protected void onAbilityResult​(int requestCode, int resultCode, [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) resultData)

Called when [startAbilityForResult(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbilityForResult-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-int-) is called to start an ability and the result is returned. 

This method is called only on Page abilities. You can start a new ability to perform some calculations and use [setResult(int,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setResult-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) to return the calculation result. Then the system calls back the current method to use the returned data to execute its own logic.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
requestCode  |  Indicates the request code returned after the ability is started. You can define the request code to identify the results returned by abilities. The value ranges from 0 to 65535.  
resultCode  |  Indicates the result code returned after the ability is started. You can define the result code to identify an error.  
resultData  |  Indicates the data returned after the ability is started. You can define the data returned. The value can be null.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onEventDispatch

public void onEventDispatch​()

Called every time a key, touch, or trackball event is dispatched to this ability. You can override this callback method if you want to know that the user has interacted with the device in a certain way while this ability is running. This method, together with [onLeaveForeground()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onLeaveForeground--), is designed to help abilities intelligently manage status bar notifications. Specifically, they help abilities determine when to cancel a notification.

#### [h2]startAbility

public final void startAbility​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent) throws [IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724), [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Starts a new ability. 

A Page or Service ability uses this method to start a specific ability. The system locates the target ability from installed abilities based on the value of the intent parameter and then starts it. You can specify the ability to start using the intent parameter.

You can start a new Service ability even if the application hosting the ability is not started by adding a flag of [Intent#FLAG_START_FOREGROUND_ABILITY](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120019__FLAG_START_FOREGROUND_ABILITY).

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the ability to start.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if the input parameter is null.  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if the ability is not initialized.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onCommand

protected void onCommand​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, boolean restart, int startId)

Called back when Service is started. 

This method can be called only by Service. You can use the [startAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to start Service. Then the system calls back the current method to use the transferred intent parameter to execute its own logic.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the intent of Service to start.  
restart  |  Indicates the startup mode. The value true indicates that Service is restarted after being destroyed, and the value false indicates a normal startup.  
startId  |  Indicates the number of times the Service ability has been started. The startId is incremented by 1 every time the ability is started. For example, if the ability has been started for six times, the value of startId is 6.  
  
**Since:**

3

Called when a touch event is dispatched to this ability. The default implementation of this callback does nothing and returns false. 

**Parameters:**

openFile​([Uri](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/uri-0000001054559009) uri, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) mode) throws [FileNotFoundException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filenotfoundexception-0000001054558439) Opens a file. This method should be implemented by a Data ability. **Parameters:** | Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
uri  |  Indicates the path of the file to open.  
mode  |  Indicates the open mode, which can be "r" for read-only access, "w" for write-only access (erasing whatever data is currently in the file), "wt" for write access that truncates any existing file, "wa" for write-only access to append to any existing data, "rw" for read and write access on any existing data, or "rwt" for read and write access that truncates any existing file.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns the [FileDescriptor](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filedescriptor-0000001054119688) object of the file descriptor.

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FileNotFoundException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filenotfoundexception-0000001054558439) |  Throws this exception if the file does not exist.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]createPipeFile

public <T> [FileDescriptor](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filedescriptor-0000001054119688) createPipeFile​([Uri](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/uri-0000001054559009) uri, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) mimeType, [PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) pacMap, T args, [PipeFileWriter](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pipefilewriter-0000001060039121)<T> pipeFileWriter) throws [FileNotFoundException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/filenotfoundexception-0000001054558439), [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Creates a data pipe, calls a background thread to write the data pointed to by the specified Uri into the pipe, and returns the FileDescriptor object used for data reading. 

[PipeFileWriter](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pipefilewriter-0000001060039121)#write(FileDescriptor, Uri, String, PacMap, T)} must have been implemented for writing data to a pipe. You should disable the FileDescriptor object returned by this method after you finish using it.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
uri  |  Indicates the source Uri object whose data will be written.  
mimeType  |  Indicates the MIME type of the data to write.  
pacMap  |  Indicates the [PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) object to be passed in this call. This parameter can be null.  
args  |  Indicates the custom parameters that specify the data shared to the client. The type of this parameter is the same as the generic type in [PipeFileWriter](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pipefilewriter-0000001060039121)}.  
pipeFileWriter  |  Indicates the instance of a class that implements [PipeFileWriter](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pipefilewriter-0000001060039121)#write(FileDescriptor, Uri, String, PacMap, T)} for pipe data writing.  
  
**Returns:**

[IAbilityStartCallback](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilitystartcallback-0000001170334517) callback) throws [IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724), [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Starts a new ability. 

A Page or Service ability uses this method to start a specific ability. The system locates the target ability from installed abilities based on the value of the intent parameter and then starts it. You can specify the ability to start using the intent parameter.

You can start a new Service ability even if the application hosting the ability is not started by adding a flag of [Intent#FLAG_START_FOREGROUND_ABILITY](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120019__FLAG_START_FOREGROUND_ABILITY).

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the ability to start.  
callback  |  Inidcates the callback to start.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if the input parameter is null.  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if the ability is not initialized.  
  
**Since:**

5

#### [h2]onMemoryLevel

public void onMemoryLevel​(int level)

Called when the system has determined to trim the memory, for example, when the ability is running in the background and there is no enough memory for running as many background processes as possible. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
level  |  Indicates the memory trim level, which shows the current memory usage status. The values and their respective meanings are as follows: 
                * 5: The application is running properly and will not be killed, but the device is running slightly low on memory. The system may start to kill processes based on the Least Recently Used (LRU) list. 
                * 10: The application is running properly and will not be killed, but the device is running extremely low on memory. Your application should release some unnecessary resources to improve system performance, which accordingly improves the application performance. 
                * 15: The application is running properly, but the system has killed most of the cached processes based on the LRU list. In this case, your application should release as many unnecessary resources as possible. Otherwise, the system may continue to kill all the cached processes and start to kill some processes that should be kept running, for example, services running in the background. 
                * 20: The application UI is hidden, for example, because the user touched the Home key on the device. Your application should releases resources allocated for UI display to free up memory. 
                * 40: The device is running low on memory and your application is around the top of the LRU list. The system is not likely to kill your application. To keep your application processes in the cache for a longer time, your application should increase available system memory by releasing some resources that can be easily restored. In this way, your application is able to smoothly return to the foreground, instead of being restarted. 
                * 60: The device is running extremely low on memory and your application is around the middle of the LRU list. Your application may be killed later if no more memory is freed up. 
                * 80: The device is running extremely low on memory and your application is at the end of the LRU list. The system is most likely to kill your application first. In this case, your application should release all resources that can be released.   
  
**Since:**

3

intent  |  Indicates the intent of the Service ability being connected.

#### [h2]onAbilityResult

protected void onAbilityResult​(int requestCode, int resultCode, [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) resultData)

Called when [startAbilityForResult(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbilityForResult-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-int-) is called to start an ability and the result is returned. 

This method is called only on Page abilities. You can start a new ability to perform some calculations and use [setResult(int,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setResult-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) to return the calculation result. Then the system calls back the current method to use the returned data to execute its own logic.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
requestCode  |  Indicates the request code returned after the ability is started. You can define the request code to identify the results returned by abilities. The value ranges from 0 to 65535.  
resultCode  |  Indicates the result code returned after the ability is started. You can define the result code to identify an error.  
resultData  |  Indicates the data returned after the ability is started. You can define the data returned. The value can be null.  
  
**Since:**

1

This change is effective since API version 5. Replaced by [onTriggerFormEvent(long,java.lang.String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onTriggerFormEvent-long-java_lang_String-). You must override this method to define the work to do after the message event is triggered if a message event is configured. You can define a message event for a component on a JS form.
