---|---  
formId  |  Indicates the ID of the form on which the message event is triggered, which is provided by the client to the form provider.  
message  |  Indicates the value of the params field of the message event. This parameter is used to identify the specific component on which the event is triggered.  
**Since:** 5 [h2]onTriggerFormEvent protected void onTriggerFormEvent​(long formId, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) message) Called when a specified message event defined by the form provider is triggered. This method is valid only for JS forms. You must override this method to define the work to do after the message event is triggered if a message event is configured. You can define a message event for a component on a JS form. When the user touches the particular component on the form, your application, as the form provider, will be notified and receive the value of the params field contained in the event. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
formId  |  Indicates the ID of the form on which the message event is triggered, which is provided by the client to the form provider.  
message  |  Indicates the value of the params field of the message event. This parameter is used to identify the specific component on which the event is triggered.

---|---

---|---  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if the form fails to be obtained due to any of the following reasons: 
                * The passed formID or formBindingData is invalid. The value of formID must be larger than 0, and formBindingData must not be null.
                * An error occurred when connecting to the Form Manager Service.
                * The specified form ID does not exist. 
                * The form has been obtained by another application and cannot be updated by the current application.
                * The form is being restored.

**[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) Starts an ability with specific start settings and returns the execution result when the ability is destroyed. When the ability is destroyed, [onAbilityResult(int,int,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onAbilityResult-int-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) is called and the returned requestCode is transferred to the current method. The given requestCode is customized and cannot be a negative number. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the ability to start.  
requestCode  |  Indicates the request code returned after the ability is started. You can define the request code to identify the results returned by abilities. The value ranges from 0 to 65535.  
abilityStartSetting  |  Indicates the setting ability used to start.  
**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if the input parameter is invalid.  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if the ability is not initialized.  
**Since:** 4 **See Also:** [startAbility(Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-), [onAbilityResult(int, int, Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onAbilityResult-int-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-), [setResult(int, Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setResult-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) [h2]startAbilityForResult public final void startAbilityForResult​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, int requestCode) throws [IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724), [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) Starts an ability and returns the execution result when the ability is destroyed. When the ability is destroyed, [onAbilityResult(int,int,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onAbilityResult-int-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) is called and the returned requestCode is transferred to the current method. The given requestCode is customized and cannot be a negative number. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the ability to start.

**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---

**Since:** 1 **See Also:** [startAbility(Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-), [onAbilityResult(int, int, Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onAbilityResult-int-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-), [setResult(int, Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setResult-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) [h2]setResult public final void setResult​(int resultCode, [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) resultData) Sets the result code and data to be returned by this Page ability to the caller. When a Page ability is destroyed, the caller overrides the [AbilitySlice#onAbilityResult(int, int, Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onAbilityResult-int-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to receive the result set in the current method. This method can be called only after the ability has been initialized. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description

---|---

#### [h2]onCreateForm

protected [ProviderFormInfo](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/providerforminfo-0000001071833380) onCreateForm​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent) Called to return a [ProviderFormInfo](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/providerforminfo-0000001071833380) object. You must override this method if your ability will serve as a form provider to provide a form for clients. The default implementation returns null.  **Parameters:** | Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the detailed information for creating a [ProviderFormInfo](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/providerforminfo-0000001071833380). The Intent object must include the form ID, form name, and grid style of the form, which can be obtained from [AbilitySlice#PARAM_FORM_IDENTITY_KEY](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__PARAM_FORM_IDENTITY_KEY), [AbilitySlice#PARAM_FORM_NAME_KEY](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__PARAM_FORM_NAME_KEY), and [AbilitySlice#PARAM_FORM_DIMENSION_KEY](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__PARAM_FORM_DIMENSION_KEY), respectively. Such form information must be managed as persistent data for further form acquisition, update, and deletion.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns the created [ProviderFormInfo](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/providerforminfo-0000001071833380) object.

**Since:**

5

#### [h2]onEventNotify

protected void onEventNotify​([Map](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/map-0000001054199858)<[Long](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/long-0000001054439731),[Integer](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/integer-0000001054119702)> formEvents)

Called when the form provider receives form events from the system. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
formEvents  |  Indicates the form events occurred. The key in the Map object indicates the form ID, and the value indicates the event type, which can be either [FORM_VISIBLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__FORM_VISIBLE) or [FORM_INVISIBLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__FORM_INVISIBLE). [FORM_VISIBLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__FORM_VISIBLE) means that the form becomes visible, and [FORM_INVISIBLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__FORM_INVISIBLE) means that the form becomes invisible.  
  
**Since:**

5

#### [h2]onTriggerFormEvent

[@Deprecated](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/deprecated-0000001054838412) protected void onTriggerFormEvent​(int formId, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) message)

Deprecated. This change is effective since API version 5. Replaced by [onTriggerFormEvent(long,java.lang.String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onTriggerFormEvent-long-java_lang_String-).

Called when a specified message event defined by the form provider is triggered. This method is valid only for JS forms. 

You must override this method to define the work to do after the message event is triggered if a message event is configured. You can define a message event for a component on a JS form. When the user touches the particular component on the form, your application, as the form provider, will be notified and receive the value of the params field contained in the event.

**Parameters:**  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]addActionRoute

public final void addActionRoute​([String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) action, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) entry) throws [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Binds an action to [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680). 

By binding an action, you can set different action parameters in [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) to present different initial pages. You must register actions in the profile file.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
action  |  Indicates the action to bind.  
entry  |  Indicates the entry, which is the fully qualified name of your [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680) class.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if this ability is not a Page ability or is not initialized.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]keepBackgroundRunning

public final void keepBackgroundRunning​(int id, [NotificationRequest](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/notificationrequest-0000001054518854) notificationRequest)

Keeps this Service ability in the background and displays a notification bar. 

To use this method, you need to request the ohos.permission.KEEP_BACKGROUND_RUNNING permission from the system. The ohos.permission.KEEP_BACKGROUND_RUNNING permission is of the normal level.

This method can be called only by Service abilities after the [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method is called.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
id  |  Identifies the notification bar information.  
notificationRequest  |  Indicates the NotificationRequest instance containing information for displaying a notification bar.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw NameReturns:** Returns true if the update is successful; returns false otherwise. **Throws:** | Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if the form fails to be obtained due to any of the following reasons: 
                * The passed formID or formBindingData is invalid. The value of formID must be larger than 0, and formBindingData must not be null.
                * An error occurred when connecting to the Form Manager Service.
                * The specified form ID does not exist. 
                * The form has been obtained by another application and cannot be updated by the current application.
                * The form is being restored.  
  
**Since:**

5

#### [h2]setFormNextRefreshTime

public boolean setFormNextRefreshTime​(long formId, long nextTime) throws [FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856)

Sets the next dynamic refresh time of a form. 

This method is provided for form providers.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
formId  |  Indicates the ID of the form for which you want to set the refresh time.  
nextTime  |  Indicates the interval between the current time and the next refresh time in seconds. The smallest value is 300.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns true if the setting is successful; returns false otherwise.

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if any of the cases occur: 
                * The value of formId is less than or equal to 0, or the value of nextTime is less than 300.
                * An error occurred when connecting to the Form Manager Service.
                * The specified form ID does not exist.
                * The form has been obtained by another application and cannot be updated by the current application.
                * The form is being restored.
                * The number of refresh times has reached the limit.  
  
**Since:**

6

#### [h2]onAcquireFormState

protected [FormState](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formstate-0000001170214413) onAcquireFormState​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent)

Called to return a [FormState](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formstate-0000001170214413) object. 

You must override this callback if you want this ability to return the actual form state. Otherwise, this method returns [FormState#DEFAULT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formstate-0000001170214413#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001170214413__DEFAULT) by default.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the description of the form for which the [FormState](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formstate-0000001170214413) is obtained. The description covers the bundle name, ability name, module name, form name, and form dimensions, which can be obtained through [ElementName#getBundleName()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/elementname-0000001054200068#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054200068__getBundleName--), [ElementName#getAbilityName()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/elementname-0000001054200068#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054200068__getAbilityName--), [AbilitySlice#PARAM_MODULE_NAME_KEY](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__PARAM_MODULE_NAME_KEY), [AbilitySlice#PARAM_FORM_NAME_KEY](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__PARAM_FORM_NAME_KEY), and [AbilitySlice#PARAM_FORM_DIMENSION_KEY](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__PARAM_FORM_DIMENSION_KEY) respectively.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns the [FormState](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formstate-0000001170214413) object.

**Since:**

6

#### [h2]onSaveAbilityState

public void onSaveAbilityState​([PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) outState)

Saves temporary data and states of this ability. This method should be implemented by a Page ability. 

This method is called when the system determines that the ability may be destroyed in an unexpected situation, for example, when the screen orientation changes or the user touches the Home key. Generally, this method is used only to save temporary states.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
outState  |  Indicates the [PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) object used for storing user data and states. This parameter cannot be null.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onStoreDataWhenConfigChange

public [Object](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446) onStoreDataWhenConfigChange​()

Called to save the data to be destroyed when the system configuration is changed. This method can return any required object, including the ability instance itself. You can call [AbilityContext.getLastStoredDataWhenConfigChanged()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054358759__getLastStoredDataWhenConfigChanged--) to obtain the saved data in a new ability instance. 

**Returns:**

Returns an object of the saved data.

**Since:**

4

#### [h2]onSetCaller

public [Uri](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/uri-0000001054559009) onSetCaller​()

Called to set caller information for the application. The default implementation returns null. 

**Returns:**

Returns the caller information.

**Since:**

4

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
formId  |  Indicates the ID of the form on which the message event is triggered, which is provided by the client to the form provider.  
message  |  Indicates the value of the params field of the message event. This parameter is used to identify the specific component on which the event is triggered.  
  
**Since:**

5

#### [h2]onTriggerFormEvent

protected void onTriggerFormEvent​(long formId, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) message)

Called when a specified message event defined by the form provider is triggered. This method is valid only for JS forms. 

You must override this method to define the work to do after the message event is triggered if a message event is configured. You can define a message event for a component on a JS form. When the user touches the particular component on the form, your application, as the form provider, will be notified and receive the value of the params field contained in the event.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
formId  |  Indicates the ID of the form on which the message event is triggered, which is provided by the client to the form provider.  
message  |  Indicates the value of the params field of the message event. This parameter is used to identify the specific component on which the event is triggered.  
  
**Since:**

5

#### [h2]updateForm

[@Deprecated](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/deprecated-0000001054838412) public boolean updateForm​(int formId, [ComponentProvider](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/componentprovider-0000001054440001) component) throws [FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856)

Deprecated. This change is effective since API version 5. Replaced by [updateForm(long,ohos.agp.components.ComponentProvider)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__updateForm-long-ohos_agp_components_ComponentProvider-).

Updates the content of a specified Java form. 

This method is called by a form provider to update Java form data as needed.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
formId  |  Indicates the ID of the Java form to update.  
component  |  Indicates the ComponentProvider object used to update the Java form displayed on the client.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns true if the update is successful; returns false otherwise.

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if the form fails to be obtained due to any of the following reasons: 
                * The passed formID or formBindingData is invalid. The value of formID must be larger than 0, and formBindingData must not be null.
                * An error occurred when connecting to the Form Manager Service.
                * The specified form ID does not exist. 
                * The form has been obtained by another application and cannot be updated by the current application.
                * The form is being restored.  
  
**Since:**

5

#### [h2]onRestoreAbilityState

public void onRestoreAbilityState​([PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) inState) Restores data and states of an ability when it is restored by the system. This method should be implemented by a Page ability. This method is called if an ability was destroyed at a certain time due to resource reclaim or was unexpectedly destroyed and the [onSaveAbilityState(ohos.utils.PacMap)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onSaveAbilityState-ohos_utils_PacMap-) method was called to save its user data and states. Generally, this method is called after the [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method. **Parameters:** | Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
inState  |  Indicates the [PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) object used for storing data and states. This parameter can not be null.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onRequestPermissionsFromUserResult

public void onRequestPermissionsFromUserResult​(int requestCode, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460)[] permissions, int[] grantResults)

Called back after permissions are requested by using [AbilityContext.requestPermissionsFromUser(java.lang.String[],int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054358759__requestPermissionsFromUser-java_lang_String:A-int-). 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
requestCode  |  Indicates the request code passed to this method from [AbilityContext.requestPermissionsFromUser(java.lang.String[],int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054358759__requestPermissionsFromUser-java_lang_String:A-int-).  
permissions  |  Indicates the list of permissions requested by using [AbilityContext.requestPermissionsFromUser(java.lang.String[],int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054358759__requestPermissionsFromUser-java_lang_String:A-int-). This parameter cannot be null.  
grantResults  |  Indicates the granting results of the corresponding permissions requested using [AbilityContext.requestPermissionsFromUser(java.lang.String[],int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054358759__requestPermissionsFromUser-java_lang_String:A-int-). The value 0 indicates that a permission is granted, and the value -1 indicates not.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onNewDescription

public [CharSequence](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/charsequence-0000001054678442) onNewDescription​()

Called before this ability is background to generate a new description for this ability. This method is designed to return a textual description of an ability's current state to be displayed to the user. 

**Returns:**

Returns a brief description of the current user behavior.

**Since:**

3

#### [h2]onLeaveForeground

public void onLeaveForeground​()

Called when this ability is about to leave the foreground and enter the background due to a user operation, for example, when the user touches the Home key. 

**Since:**

3

#### [h2]onEventDispatch

public void onEventDispatch​()

Called every time a key, touch, or trackball event is dispatched to this ability. You can override this callback method if you want to know that the user has interacted with the device in a certain way while this ability is running. This method, together with [onLeaveForeground()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onLeaveForeground--), is designed to help abilities intelligently manage status bar notifications. Specifically, they help abilities determine when to cancel a notification. 

**Since:**

3

#### [h2]startAbility

public final void startAbility​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent) throws [IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724), [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Starts a new ability. 

A Page or Service ability uses this method to start a specific ability. The system locates the target ability from installed abilities based on the value of the intent parameter and then starts it. You can specify the ability to start using the intent parameter.

You can start a new Service ability even if the application hosting the ability is not started by adding a flag of [Intent#FLAG_START_FOREGROUND_ABILITY](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120019__FLAG_START_FOREGROUND_ABILITY).

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the ability to start.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if the input parameter is null.  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if the ability is not initialized.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]startAbility

public final void startAbility​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, [IAbilityStartCallback](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilitystartcallback-0000001170334517) callback) throws [IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724), [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Starts a new ability. 

A Page or Service ability uses this method to start a specific ability. The system locates the target ability from installed abilities based on the value of the intent parameter and then starts it. You can specify the ability to start using the intent parameter.

You can start a new Service ability even if the application hosting the ability is not started by adding a flag of [Intent#FLAG_START_FOREGROUND_ABILITY](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120019__FLAG_START_FOREGROUND_ABILITY).

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the ability to start.  
callback  |  Inidcates the callback to start.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[ | Throw Description  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if this ability is not a Service ability or is not initialized.  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception in the passed NotificationRequest instance is null.  
[ProfileMissingConfigException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/profilemissingconfigexception-0000001054678653) |  Throws this exception if param backgroundModes is not set in profile.  
**Since:** 1 [h2]cancelBackgroundRunning public final void cancelBackgroundRunning​() throws [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) Cancels background running of this ability to free up system memory. This method can be called only by Service abilities when the [onStop()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStop--) method is called. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if this ability is not a Service ability or is not initialized.  
**Since:** 1 [h2]continueAbility public final void continueAbility​() throws [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452), [UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) Migrates this ability to another device on the same distributed network. If there are multiple candidate devices, a pop-up will be displayed for users to choose the desired one.  The ability to migrate and its ability slices must implement the [IAbilityContinuation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilitycontinuation-0000001054358657) interface. Otherwise, an exception is thrown, indicating that the ability does not support migration. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if this ability is not initialized or is in a state where this operation is not allowed, for example, it is being migrated.  
[UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) |  Throws this exception if this ability does not implement the IAbilityContinuation interface.  
**Since:** 1 [h2]continueAbility public final void continueAbility​([String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) deviceId) throws [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452), [UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) Migrates this ability to the given device on the same distributed network. The ability to migrate and its ability slices must implement the [IAbilityContinuation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilitycontinuation-0000001054358657) interface. Otherwise, an exception is thrown, indicating that the ability does not support migration. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
deviceId  |  Indicates the ID of the target device where this ability will be migrated to. If this parameter is null, this method has the same effect as [continueAbility()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__continueAbility--).  
**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if this ability is not initialized or is in a state where this operation is not allowed, for example, it is being migrated.  
[UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) |  Throws this exception if this ability does not implement the IAbilityContinuation interface.  
**Since:** 3 [h2]continueAbilityReversibly public final void continueAbilityReversibly​() throws [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452), [UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) Migrates this ability to another device on the same distributed network in a reversible way that allows this ability to be migrated back to the local device through [reverseContinueAbility()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__reverseContinueAbility--). Otherwise, an exception is thrown, indicating that the ability does not support migration. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if this ability is not initialized or is in a state where this operation is not allowed, for example, it is being migrated.  
[UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) |  Throws this exception if this ability does not implement the IAbilityContinuation interface.

#### [h2]startAbility

public final void startAbility​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, [AbilityStartSetting](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitystartsetting-0000001060519066) abilityStartSetting) throws [IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724), [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Starts a new ability with specific start settings. 

A Page or Service ability uses this method to start a specific ability. The system locates the target ability from installed abilities based on the value of the intent parameter and then starts it. You can specify the ability to start using the intent parameter.

You can start a new Service ability even if the application hosting the ability is not started by adding a flag of [Intent#FLAG_START_FOREGROUND_ABILITY](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120019__FLAG_START_FOREGROUND_ABILITY).

**Parameters:**

---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the ability to start.  
abilityStartSetting  |  Indicates the setting ability used to start.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if the input parameter is null.  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if the ability is not initialized.  
  
**Since:**

4

#### [h2]startAbility

public final void startAbility​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, [AbilityStartSetting](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitystartsetting-0000001060519066) abilityStartSetting, [AbilityTransitionUtils](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitytransitionutils-0000001217996499) abilityTransitionUtils) throws [IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724), [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Starts a new ability with specific start settings. 

A Page or Service ability uses this method to start a specific ability. The system locates the target ability from installed abilities based on the value of the intent parameter and then starts it. You can specify the ability to start using the intent parameter.

You can start a new Service ability even if the application hosting the ability is not started by adding a flag of [Intent#FLAG_START_FOREGROUND_ABILITY](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120019__FLAG_START_FOREGROUND_ABILITY).

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the ability to start.  
abilityStartSetting  |  Indicates the setting ability used to start.  
abilityTransitionUtils  |  Indicates the setting ability used to start.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if the input parameter is null.  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if the ability is not initialized.  
  
**Since:**

7

#### [h2]startAbilityForResult

public final void startAbilityForResult​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, int requestCode, [AbilityStartSetting](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitystartsetting-0000001060519066) abilityStartSetting) throws [IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724), [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Starts an ability with specific start settings and returns the execution result when the ability is destroyed. 

When the ability is destroyed, [onAbilityResult(int,int,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onAbilityResult-int-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) is called and the returned requestCode is transferred to the current method. The given requestCode is customized and cannot be a negative number.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the ability to start.  
requestCode  |  Indicates the request code returned after the ability is started. You can define the request code to identify the results returned by abilities. The value ranges from 0 to 65535.  
abilityStartSetting  |  Indicates the setting ability used to start.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if the input parameter is invalid.  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if the ability is not initialized.  
arg  |  Indicates the [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) parameter transferred by the method.  
extras  |  Indicates the [PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) parameter transferred by the method.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns the PacMap result of the method.

**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onStart

protected void onStart​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent)

Called back when an ability is started for initialization (it can be called only once in the entire lifecycle of an ability). 

Most initialization operations should be performed by calling this method. For example, you can use the current method to call [setMainRoute(java.lang.String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setMainRoute-java_lang_String-) and [addActionRoute(java.lang.String,java.lang.String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__addActionRoute-java_lang_String-java_lang_String-) to set the main router and action router for a Page ability.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates information carried when this ability is started. **Note: For a Service ability, null is passed.**  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onPostStart

protected void onPostStart​([PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) pacMap)

Called when the ability has been started by calling [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) and [onRestoreAbilityState(ohos.utils.PacMap)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onRestoreAbilityState-ohos_utils_PacMap-). 

Generally, this method is used for system classes to perform final initialization operations after an application starts. You do not need to implement this method for your application. When overriding this method, you must call the implementation of its parent class. Otherwise, an exception will be thrown.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
pacMap  |  Indicates the data most recently provided in [onSaveAbilityState(ohos.utils.PacMap)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onSaveAbilityState-ohos_utils_PacMap-).  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]onStop

protected void onStop​()

Called back before an ability is destroyed. 

This method indicates the end of the ability lifecycle. It can be executed after the ability calls the [terminateAbility()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__terminateAbility--) method or the last ability slice of the ability calls the [AbilitySlice#terminate()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__terminate--) method. Generally, this method is used to clear resources.

**Since:**

1

Called back when an ability enters the **ACTIVE** state. 

The ability in the **ACTIVE** state is in the foreground and is interactive.

**Since:**

1

#### [h2]startAbilityForResult

public final void startAbilityForResult​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, int requestCode) throws [IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724), [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Starts an ability and returns the execution result when the ability is destroyed. 

When the ability is destroyed, [onAbilityResult(int,int,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onAbilityResult-int-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) is called and the returned requestCode is transferred to the current method. The given requestCode is customized and cannot be a negative number.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the ability to start.  
requestCode  |  Indicates the request code returned after the ability is started. You can define the request code to identify the results returned by abilities. The value ranges from 0 to 65535.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if the input parameter is invalid.  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if the ability is not initialized.  
  
**Since:**

1

**See Also:**

[startAbility(Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-), [onAbilityResult(int, int, Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onAbilityResult-int-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-), [setResult(int, Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setResult-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-)

#### [h2]setResult

public final void setResult​(int resultCode, [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) resultData)

Sets the result code and data to be returned by this Page ability to the caller. 

When a Page ability is destroyed, the caller overrides the [AbilitySlice#onAbilityResult(int, int, Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onAbilityResult-int-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to receive the result set in the current method. This method can be called only after the ability has been initialized.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
resultCode  |  Indicates the result code returned after the ability is destroyed. You can define the result code to identify an error.  
resultData  |  Indicates the data returned after the ability is destroyed. You can define the data returned. This parameter can be null.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if this ability is not a Page ability or the ability is not initialized.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]terminateAbility

public final void terminateAbility​()

Destroys this Page or Service ability. 

After a Page or Service ability performs all operations, it can use this method to destroy itself to free up memory. This method can be called only after the ability is initialized.

**Specified by:**

[terminateAbility](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/context-0000001054440065#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054440065__terminateAbility--) in interface [Context](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/context-0000001054440065)

**Overrides:**

[terminateAbility](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054358759__terminateAbility--) in class [AbilityContext](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759)

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if the ability is not initialized.  
  
**Since:**

1

**See Also:**

[Context.terminateAbility()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/context-0000001054440065#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054440065__terminateAbility--)

#### [h2]setMainRoute

public final void setMainRoute​([String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) entry) throws [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Sets the main route of this ability. 

You can call this method to set the default route of the ability, that is, the [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680) to present. This method can be used only for Page abilities.

**Note:** If you do not set a default route in [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-), the startup will fail.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
entry  |  Indicates the main entry, which is the fully qualified name of your [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680) class.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if this ability is not a Page ability or is not initialized.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]addActionRoute

public final void addActionRoute​([String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) action, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) entry) throws [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Binds an action to [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680). 

By binding an action, you can set different action parameters in [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) to present different initial pages. You must register actions in the profile file.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
action  |  Indicates the action to bind.  
entry  |  Indicates the entry, which is the fully qualified name of your [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680) class.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if the form fails to be obtained due to any of the following reasons: 
                * The passed formID or component is invalid. The value of formID must be larger than 0, and component must not be null.
                * An error occurred when connecting to the Form Manager Service.
                * The specified form ID does not exist. 
                * The form has been obtained by another application and cannot be updated by the current application.
                * The form is being restored.  
  
**Since:**

5

#### [h2]setFormNextRefreshTime

public boolean setFormNextRefreshTime​(long formId, long nextTime) throws [FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856)

Sets the next dynamic refresh time of a form. 

This method is provided for form providers.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
formId  |  Indicates the ID of the form for which you want to set the refresh time.  
nextTime  |  Indicates the interval between the current time and the next refresh time in seconds. The smallest value is 300.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns true if the setting is successful; returns false otherwise.

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if any of the cases occur: 
                * The value of formId is less than or equal to 0, or the value of nextTime is less than 300.
                * An error occurred when connecting to the Form Manager Service.
                * The specified form ID does not exist.
                * The form has been obtained by another application and cannot be updated by the current application.
                * The form is being restored.
                * The number of refresh times has reached the limit.  
  
**Since:**

6

#### [h2]onAcquireFormState

protected [FormState](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formstate-0000001170214413) onAcquireFormState​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent)

Called to return a [FormState](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formstate-0000001170214413) object. 

You must override this callback if you want this ability to return the actual form state. Otherwise, this method returns [FormState#DEFAULT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formstate-0000001170214413#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001170214413__DEFAULT) by default.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the description of the form for which the [FormState](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formstate-0000001170214413) is obtained. The description covers the bundle name, ability name, module name, form name, and form dimensions, which can be obtained through [ElementName#getBundleName()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/elementname-0000001054200068#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054200068__getBundleName--), [ElementName#getAbilityName()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/elementname-0000001054200068#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054200068__getAbilityName--), [AbilitySlice#PARAM_MODULE_NAME_KEY](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__PARAM_MODULE_NAME_KEY), [AbilitySlice#PARAM_FORM_NAME_KEY](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__PARAM_FORM_NAME_KEY), and [AbilitySlice#PARAM_FORM_DIMENSION_KEY](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__PARAM_FORM_DIMENSION_KEY) respectively.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns the [FormState](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formstate-0000001170214413) object.

**Since:**

6

#### [h2]keepBackgroundRunning

public final void keepBackgroundRunning​(int id, [NotificationRequest](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/notificationrequest-0000001054518854) notificationRequest)

Keeps this Service ability in the background and displays a notification bar. 

To use this method, you need to request the ohos.permission.KEEP_BACKGROUND_RUNNING permission from the system. The ohos.permission.KEEP_BACKGROUND_RUNNING permission is of the normal level.

This method can be called only by Service abilities after the [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method is called.

---|---

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if this ability is not a Service ability or is not initialized.  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception in the passed NotificationRequest instance is null.  
[ProfileMissingConfigException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/profilemissingconfigexception-0000001054678653) |  Throws this exception if param backgroundModes is not set in profile.  
  
**Since:**

#### [h2]onPostActive

Called when the ability is completely activated after calling [onActive()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onActive--).

#### [h2]onInactive

protected void onInactive​()

Called back when an ability enters the **INACTIVE** state (an ability in this state is not interactive and may change to the **BACKGROUND** or **ACTIVE** state). 

**INACTIVE** is an intermediate state and does not last for a long period of time. Frequent switching between **ACTIVE** and **INACTIVE** is possible. Therefore, you can use this method to perform certain simple operations, for example, committing any changes.

**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onForeground

protected void onForeground​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent)

Called back when the state of an ability changes from **BACKGROUND** to **INACTIVE**. 

This is an intermediate state callback method. Generally, the system calls this method to refresh data status and then calls back the [onActive()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onActive--) method.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates information carried when the ability returns to the **INACTIVE** state.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onBackground

protected void onBackground​()

Called back when an ability enters the **BACKGROUND** state. 

A Page ability in the **BACKGROUND** state is invisible. You are advised to suspend threads related to the ability and clear some resources to free up system memory.

**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onConnect

protected [IRemoteObject](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iremoteobject-0000001054678970) onConnect​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent)

This method is called only on Service abilities. You can override the method to return the proxy of a Service ability.

#### [h2]onCommand

[@Deprecated](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/deprecated-0000001054838412) protected void onCommand​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, boolean restart)

Deprecated. This change is effective since API version 3. Replaced by [onCommand(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,boolean,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onCommand-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-boolean-int-).

Called back when a Service ability is started. 

This method can be called only by Service abilities. You can use the [startAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to start a Service ability. Then the system calls back the current method to use the transferred intent parameter to execute its own logic.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the intent of the Service ability to start.  
restart  |  Indicates the startup mode. The value true indicates that the Service ability is restarted after being destroyed, and the value false indicates a normal startup.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onCommand

protected void onCommand​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, boolean restart, int startId)

Called back when Service is started. 

This method can be called only by Service. You can use the [startAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to start Service. Then the system calls back the current method to use the transferred intent parameter to execute its own logic.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  | |  Throw Description  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if this ability is not initialized or is in a state where this operation is not allowed, for example, it is being migrated.  
[UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) |  Throws this exception if this ability does not implement the IAbilityContinuation interface.  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]reverseContinueAbility

public final boolean reverseContinueAbility​() throws [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452), [UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453)

Migrates this ability from another device on the same distributed network back to the local device. 

**Returns:**

Returns true if the migration request is successful; returns false otherwise.

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if this ability was not involved in a reversible migration process, or if it is in a state where this operation is not allowed, for example, it is being migrated back.  
[UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) |  Throws this exception if this ability does not implement the IAbilityContinuation interface.  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]getOriginalDeviceId

public final [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) getOriginalDeviceId​() throws [UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453)

Obtains the ID of the source device from which this ability is migrated. 

You can call this method on the target remote device to obtain the source device ID after you have initiated a reversible migration process that allows the ability to be migrated back from the target device to the source device.

**Returns:**

Returns the source device ID.

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) |  Throws this exception if this ability does not implement the IAbilityContinuation interface.  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]setAbilitySliceAnimator

public final void setAbilitySliceAnimator​([AbilitySliceAnimator](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitysliceanimator-0000001054358659) abilitySliceAnimator) throws [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Sets the transition animation among [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680) instances of the current ability. 

You can use the [AbilitySliceAnimator](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitysliceanimator-0000001054358659) class to define animation properties. Currently, only translation is supported. Rotation, gradient, and scale operations will be supported later. The properties can be set by calling the [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method of the ability.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
abilitySliceAnimator  |  Indicates the animation properties, such as translate, rotate, or alpha. If this parameter is null, the transition animation is disabled.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if the ability is not initialized.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]isTerminating

public boolean isTerminating​()

Checks whether the current ability is being destroyed. 

An ability is being destroyed if you called [terminateAbility()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__terminateAbility--) on it or someone else requested to destroy it.

**Returns:**

Returns true if the current ability is being destroyed; returns false otherwise.

**Since:**

3

**See Also:**

[terminateAbility()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__terminateAbility--)

#### [h2]setIntent

public void setIntent​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent)

Sets the Intent object that can be obtained by calling [getIntent()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__getIntent--). 

This method does not copy the given Intent object, instead, it holds a reference to the given object and takes effect directly on it. This method is often used with [onNewIntent(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onNewIntent-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-).

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the Intent object to set.  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]onWindowFocusChanged

public void onWindowFocusChanged​(boolean hasFocus)

Called when this ability gains or loses window focus. 

The focus state refers to the global state, which is independent of the ability lifecycle states. Although the lifecycle state change of an ability may lead to a focus change (for example, [onStop()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStop--) may cause the ability to lose window focus), there is no particular sequence between this callback and other lifecycle callbacks such as [onBackground()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onBackground--). 

Generally, the ability in the foreground will have window focus. The ability will lose focus when another dialog box or pop-up window is displayed. The ability will also lose focus when a system-level window (such as the status bar or a system alarm) is displayed.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
hasFocus  |  Specifies whether this ability has focus.  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]normalizeUri

public [Uri](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/uri-0000001054559009) normalizeUri​([Uri](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/uri-0000001054559009) uri)

Converts the given uri that refer to the Data ability into a normalized URI. A normalized URI can be used across devices, persisted, backed up, and restored. It can refer to the same item in the Data ability even if the context has changed. 

If you implement URI normalization for a Data ability, you must also implement [denormalizeUri(ohos.utils.net.Uri)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__denormalizeUri-ohos_utils_net_Uri-) to enable URI denormalization. After this feature is enabled, URIs passed to any method that is called on the Data ability must require normalization verification and denormalization. 

The default implementation of this method returns null, indicating that this Data ability does not support URI normalization.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
uri  |  Indicates the [Uri](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/uri-0000001054559009) object to normalize.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns the normalized Uri object if the Data ability supports URI normalization; returns null otherwise.

**Since:**

3

**See Also:**

[denormalizeUri(ohos.utils.net.Uri)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__denormalizeUri-ohos_utils_net_Uri-)

#### [h2]denormalizeUri

public [Uri](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/uri-0000001054559009) denormalizeUri​([Uri](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/uri-0000001054559009) uri)

Converts the given normalized uri generated by [normalizeUri(ohos.utils.net.Uri)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__normalizeUri-ohos_utils_net_Uri-) into a denormalized one. The default implementation of this method returns the original URI passed to it. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
uri  |  uri Indicates the [Uri](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/uri-0000001054559009) object to denormalize.  
outState)

Saves temporary data and states of this ability. This method should be implemented by a Page ability. 

This method is called when the system determines that the ability may be destroyed in an unexpected situation, for example, when the screen orientation changes or the user touches the Home key. Generally, this method is used only to save temporary states.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
outState  |  Indicates the [PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) object used for storing user data and states. This parameter cannot be null.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onRestoreAbilityState

public void onRestoreAbilityState​([PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) inState)

Restores data and states of an ability when it is restored by the system. This method should be implemented by a Page ability. 

This method is called if an ability was destroyed at a certain time due to resource reclaim or was unexpectedly destroyed and the [onSaveAbilityState(ohos.utils.PacMap)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onSaveAbilityState-ohos_utils_PacMap-) method was called to save its user data and states. Generally, this method is called after the [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
inState  |  Indicates the [PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) object used for storing data and states. This parameter can not be null.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onRequestPermissionsFromUserResult

public void onRequestPermissionsFromUserResult​(int requestCode, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460)[] permissions, int[] grantResults)

Called back after permissions are requested by using [AbilityContext.requestPermissionsFromUser(java.lang.String[],int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054358759__requestPermissionsFromUser-java_lang_String:A-int-).

Parameter Name | Parameter Description

permissions  |  Indicates the list of permissions requested by using [AbilityContext.requestPermissionsFromUser(java.lang.String[],int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054358759__requestPermissionsFromUser-java_lang_String:A-int-). This parameter cannot be null.  
grantResults  |  Indicates the granting results of the corresponding permissions requested using [AbilityContext.requestPermissionsFromUser(java.lang.String[],int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054358759__requestPermissionsFromUser-java_lang_String:A-int-).
