#### [h2]onEventDispatch

public void onEventDispatch​()

Called every time a key, touch, or trackball event is dispatched to this ability. You can override this callback method if you want to know that the user has interacted with the device in a certain way while this ability is running. This method, together with [onLeaveForeground()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onLeaveForeground--), is designed to help abilities intelligently manage status bar notifications. Specifically, they help abilities determine when to cancel a notification. 

**Since:**

3

#### [h2]startAbility

public final void startAbility​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent) throws [IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724), [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Starts a new ability. 

A Page or Service ability uses this method to start a specific ability. The system locates the target ability from installed abilities based on the value of the intent parameter and then starts it. You can specify the ability to start using the intent parameter.

You can start a new Service ability even if the application hosting the ability is not started by adding a flag of [Intent#FLAG_START_FOREGROUND_ABILITY](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120019__FLAG_START_FOREGROUND_ABILITY).

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the ability to start.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if the input parameter is null.  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if the ability is not initialized.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]startAbility

public final void startAbility​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, [IAbilityStartCallback](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilitystartcallback-0000001170334517) callback) throws [IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724), [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Starts a new ability. 

A Page or Service ability uses this method to start a specific ability. The system locates the target ability from installed abilities based on the value of the intent parameter and then starts it. You can specify the ability to start using the intent parameter.

You can start a new Service ability even if the application hosting the ability is not started by adding a flag of [Intent#FLAG_START_FOREGROUND_ABILITY](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120019__FLAG_START_FOREGROUND_ABILITY).

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|--- Indicates the intent of Service to start.  
  
restart  |  Indicates the startup mode. The value true indicates that Service is restarted after being destroyed, and the value false indicates a normal startup.  
startId  |  Indicates the number of times the Service ability has been started. The startId is incremented by 1 every time the ability is started. For example, if the ability has been started for six times, the value of startId is 6.  
  
**Since:**

3

protected void onOrientationChanged​([AbilityInfo.DisplayOrientation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810) displayOrientation)

Called back when the display orientation of a screen changes.

displayOrientation  |  Indicates the new display orientation. The following values are supported: [AbilityInfo.DisplayOrientation#LANDSCAPE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054518810__LANDSCAPE), [AbilityInfo.DisplayOrientation#PORTRAIT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054518810__PORTRAIT), [AbilityInfo.DisplayOrientation#FOLLOWRECENT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054518810__FOLLOWRECENT), and [AbilityInfo.DisplayOrientation#UNSPECIFIED](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054518810__UNSPECIFIED).  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]onMemoryLevel

public void onMemoryLevel​(int level)

Called when the system has determined to trim the memory, for example, when the ability is running in the background and there is no enough memory for running as many background processes as possible. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
level  |  Indicates the memory trim level, which shows the current memory usage status. The values and their respective meanings are as follows: 
                * 5: The application is running properly and will not be killed, but the device is running slightly low on memory. The system may start to kill processes based on the Least Recently Used (LRU) list. 
                * 10: The application is running properly and will not be killed, but the device is running extremely low on memory. Your application should release some unnecessary resources to improve system performance, which accordingly improves the application performance. 
                * 15: The application is running properly, but the system has killed most of the cached processes based on the LRU list. In this case, your application should release as many unnecessary resources as possible. Otherwise, the system may continue to kill all the cached processes and start to kill some processes that should be kept running, for example, services running in the background. 
                * 20: The application UI is hidden, for example, because the user touched the Home key on the device. Your application should releases resources allocated for UI display to free up memory. 
                * 40: The device is running low on memory and your application is around the top of the LRU list. The system is not likely to kill your application. To keep your application processes in the cache for a longer time, your application should increase available system memory by releasing some resources that can be easily restored. In this way, your application is able to smoothly return to the foreground, instead of being restarted. 
                * 60: The device is running extremely low on memory and your application is around the middle of the LRU list. Your application may be killed later if no more memory is freed up. 
                * 80: The device is running extremely low on memory and your application is at the end of the LRU list. The system is most likely to kill your application first. In this case, your application should release all resources that can be released.   
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]onReconnect

public void onReconnect​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent)

Called when a new client attempts to connect to a Service ability after all previous client connections to it are disconnected. 

The Service ability must have been started but not been destroyed, that is, [startAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) has been called but [stopAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__stopAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) has not.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the intent of the Service ability being connected.  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]onAbilityResult

protected void onAbilityResult​(int requestCode, int resultCode, [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) resultData)

Called when [startAbilityForResult(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbilityForResult-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-int-) is called to start an ability and the result is returned. 

This method is called only on Page abilities. You can start a new ability to perform some calculations and use [setResult(int,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setResult-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) to return the calculation result. Then the system calls back the current method to use the returned data to execute its own logic.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
requestCode  |  Indicates the request code returned after the ability is started. You can define the request code to identify the results returned by abilities. The value ranges from 0 to 65535.  
resultCode  |  Indicates the result code returned after the ability is started. You can define the result code to identify an error.  
resultData  | 

#### [h2]continueAbility

public final void continueAbility​() throws [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452), [UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) Migrates this ability to another device on the same distributed network. If there are multiple candidate devices, a pop-up will be displayed for users to choose the desired one.  The ability to migrate and its ability slices must implement the [IAbilityContinuation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilitycontinuation-0000001054358657) interface. Otherwise, an exception is thrown, indicating that the ability does not support migration. **Throws:** | Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if this ability is not initialized or is in a state where this operation is not allowed, for example, it is being migrated.  
[UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) |  Throws this exception if this ability does not implement the IAbilityContinuation interface.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]continueAbility

public final void continueAbility​([String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) deviceId) throws [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452), [UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453)

Migrates this ability to the given device on the same distributed network. 

The ability to migrate and its ability slices must implement the [IAbilityContinuation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilitycontinuation-0000001054358657) interface. Otherwise, an exception is thrown, indicating that the ability does not support migration.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
deviceId  |  Indicates the ID of the target device where this ability will be migrated to. If this parameter is null, this method has the same effect as [continueAbility()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__continueAbility--).  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if this ability is not initialized or is in a state where this operation is not allowed, for example, it is being migrated.  
[UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) |  Throws this exception if this ability does not implement the IAbilityContinuation interface.  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]continueAbilityReversibly

public final void continueAbilityReversibly​() throws [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452), [UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453)

Migrates this ability to another device on the same distributed network in a reversible way that allows this ability to be migrated back to the local device through [reverseContinueAbility()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__reverseContinueAbility--). 

If there are multiple candidate devices, a pop-up will be displayed for users to choose the desired one. 

The ability to migrate and its ability slices must implement the [IAbilityContinuation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilitycontinuation-0000001054358657) interface. Otherwise, an exception is thrown, indicating that the ability does not support migration.

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if this ability is not initialized or is in a state where this operation is not allowed, for example, it is being migrated.  
[UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) |  Throws this exception if this ability does not implement the IAbilityContinuation interface.  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]continueAbilityReversibly

public final void continueAbilityReversibly​([String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) deviceId) throws [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452), [UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453)

Migrates this ability to the given device on the same distributed network in a reversible way that allows this ability to be migrated back to the local device through [reverseContinueAbility()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__reverseContinueAbility--). 

The ability to migrate and its ability slices must implement the [IAbilityContinuation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilitycontinuation-0000001054358657) interface. Otherwise, an exception is thrown, indicating that the ability does not support migration.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
deviceId  |  Indicates the ID of the target device where this ability will be migrated to. If this parameter is null, this method has the same effect as [continueAbilityReversibly()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__continueAbilityReversibly--).  
  
**Throws:**

intent  |  Indicates the ability to start.  
callback  |  Inidcates the callback to start.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if the input parameter is null.  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if the ability is not initialized.  
  
**Since:**

5

Called when this ability is moved to or removed from the top of the stack.

#### [h2]startAbility

public final void startAbility​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, [AbilityStartSetting](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitystartsetting-0000001060519066) abilityStartSetting) throws [IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724), [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Starts a new ability with specific start settings. 

A Page or Service ability uses this method to start a specific ability. The system locates the target ability from installed abilities based on the value of the intent parameter and then starts it. You can specify the ability to start using the intent parameter.

You can start a new Service ability even if the application hosting the ability is not started by adding a flag of [Intent#FLAG_START_FOREGROUND_ABILITY](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120019__FLAG_START_FOREGROUND_ABILITY).

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the ability to start.  
abilityStartSetting  |  Indicates the setting ability used to start.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if the input parameter is null.  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if the ability is not initialized.  
  
**Since:**

4

#### [h2]startAbilityForResult

public final void startAbilityForResult​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, int requestCode, [AbilityStartSetting](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitystartsetting-0000001060519066) abilityStartSetting) throws [IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724), [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Starts an ability with specific start settings and returns the execution result when the ability is destroyed. 

When the ability is destroyed, [onAbilityResult(int,int,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onAbilityResult-int-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) is called and the returned requestCode is transferred to the current method. The given requestCode is customized and cannot be a negative number.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the ability to start.  
requestCode  |  Indicates the request code returned after the ability is started. You can define the request code to identify the results returned by abilities. The value ranges from 0 to 65535.  
abilityStartSetting  |  Indicates the setting ability used to start.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if the input parameter is invalid.  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if the ability is not initialized.  
  
**Since:**

4

**See Also:**

[startAbility(Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-), [onAbilityResult(int, int, Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onAbilityResult-int-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-), [setResult(int, Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setResult-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-)

The given requestCode is customized and cannot be a negative number.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the ability to start.  
requestCode  |  Indicates the request code returned after the ability is started. You can define the request code to identify the results returned by abilities. The value ranges from 0 to 65535.

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---

**Since:**

1

**See Also:**

[startAbility(Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-), [onAbilityResult(int, int, Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onAbilityResult-int-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-), [setResult(int, Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setResult-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-)

#### [h2]setResult

public final void setResult​(int resultCode, [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) resultData)

Sets the result code and data to be returned by this Page ability to the caller. 

When a Page ability is destroyed, the caller overrides the [AbilitySlice#onAbilityResult(int, int, Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onAbilityResult-int-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to receive the result set in the current method. This method can be called only after the ability has been initialized.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
resultCode  |  Indicates the result code returned after the ability is destroyed. You can define the result code to identify an error.  
resultData  |  Indicates the data returned after the ability is destroyed. You can define the data returned. This parameter can be null.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if this ability is not a Page ability or the ability is not initialized.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]terminateAbility

public final void terminateAbility​()

Destroys this Page or Service ability. 

After a Page or Service ability performs all operations, it can use this method to destroy itself to free up memory. This method can be called only after the ability is initialized.

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if the ability is not initialized.  
  
**Since:**

1

**See Also:**

[Context.terminateAbility()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/context-0000001054440065#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054440065__terminateAbility--)

formId  |  Indicates the ID of the form.

5

#### [h2]onTriggerFormEvent

[@Deprecated](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/deprecated-0000001054838412) protected void onTriggerFormEvent​(int formId, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) message)

Deprecated. This change is effective since API version 5. Replaced by [onTriggerFormEvent(long,java.lang.String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onTriggerFormEvent-long-java_lang_String-).

Called when a specified message event defined by the form provider is triggered. This method is valid only for JS forms. 

You must override this method to define the work to do after the message event is triggered if a message event is configured. You can define a message event for a component on a JS form. When the user touches the particular component on the form, your application, as the form provider, will be notified and receive the value of the params field contained in the event.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
formId  |  Indicates the ID of the form on which the message event is triggered, which is provided by the client to the form provider.  
message  |  Indicates the value of the params field of the message event. This parameter is used to identify the specific component on which the event is triggered.  
  
**Since:**

5

#### [h2]onTriggerFormEvent

protected void onTriggerFormEvent​(long formId, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) message)

Called when a specified message event defined by the form provider is triggered. This method is valid only for JS forms. 

You must override this method to define the work to do after the message event is triggered if a message event is configured. You can define a message event for a component on a JS form. When the user touches the particular component on the form, your application, as the form provider, will be notified and receive the value of the params field contained in the event.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
formId  |  Indicates the ID of the form on which the message event is triggered, which is provided by the client to the form provider.  
message  |  Indicates the value of the params field of the message event. This parameter is used to identify the specific component on which the event is triggered.  
  
**Since:**

5

#### [h2]updateForm

[@Deprecated](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/deprecated-0000001054838412) public boolean updateForm​(int formId, [ComponentProvider](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/componentprovider-0000001054440001) component) throws [FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856)

Deprecated. This change is effective since API version 5. Replaced by [updateForm(long,ohos.agp.components.ComponentProvider)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__updateForm-long-ohos_agp_components_ComponentProvider-).

Updates the content of a specified Java form. 

This method is called by a form provider to update Java form data as needed.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
formId  |  Indicates the ID of the Java form to update.  
component  |  Indicates the ComponentProvider object used to update the Java form displayed on the client.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns true if the update is successful; returns false otherwise.

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if the form fails to be obtained due to any of the following reasons: 
                * The passed formID or formBindingData is invalid. The value of formID must be larger than 0, and formBindingData must not be null.
                * An error occurred when connecting to the Form Manager Service.
                * The specified form ID does not exist. 
                * The form has been obtained by another application and cannot be updated by the current application.
                * The form is being restored.  
  
**Since:**

5

#### [h2]setMainRoute

public final void setMainRoute​([String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) entry) throws [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Sets the main route of this ability. 

You can call this method to set the default route of the ability, that is, the [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680) to present. This method can be used only for Page abilities.

**Note:** If you do not set a default route in [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-), the startup will fail.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
entry  |  Indicates the main entry, which is the fully qualified name of your [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680) class.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if this ability is not a Page ability or is not initialized.  
  
**Since:**

1

**Parameters:**

action  |  Indicates the action to bind.  
entry  |  Indicates the entry, which is the fully qualified name of your [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680) class.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if this ability is not a Page ability or is not initialized.  
  
**Since:**

1

Obtains the ID of the source device from which this ability is migrated. 

You can call this method on the target remote device to obtain the source device ID after you have initiated a reversible migration process that allows the ability to be migrated back from the target device to the source device.

---|---

#### [h2]setAbilitySliceAnimator

public final void setAbilitySliceAnimator​([AbilitySliceAnimator](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitysliceanimator-0000001054358659) abilitySliceAnimator) throws [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Sets the transition animation among [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680) instances of the current ability. 

You can use the [AbilitySliceAnimator](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitysliceanimator-0000001054358659) class to define animation properties. Currently, only translation is supported. Rotation, gradient, and scale operations will be supported later. The properties can be set by calling the [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method of the ability.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
abilitySliceAnimator  |  Indicates the animation properties, such as translate, rotate, or alpha. If this parameter is null, the transition animation is disabled.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---

This method can be called only by Service abilities when the [onStop()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStop--) method is called. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if this ability is not a Service ability or is not initialized.  
**Since:** 1 [h2]continueAbility public final void continueAbility​() throws [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452), [UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) Migrates this ability to another device on the same distributed network. If there are multiple candidate devices, a pop-up will be displayed for users to choose the desired one.  The ability to migrate and its ability slices must implement the [IAbilityContinuation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilitycontinuation-0000001054358657) interface. Otherwise, an exception is thrown, indicating that the ability does not support migration. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
| refResults  |  Indicates the result set of database operations. This parameter cannot be null.  
operation  |  Indicates the database operation. This parameter cannot be null.  
numRefs  |  Indicates the index of a valid database operation specified by operation. The index is used to locate a particular database operation result in refResults.  
**Returns:** Returns the [ValuesBucket](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/valuesbucket-0000001054678818) object parsed from the specified database operation result. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[OperationExecuteException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/operationexecuteexception-0000001054238683) |  Throws this exception if an input parameter is invalid.

**Returns:** Returns the [DataAbilityPredicates](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dataabilitypredicates-0000001054200074) object parsed from the specified database operation result. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---

**Since:** 4 [h2]startAssistAbility public final void startAssistAbility​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, [IAssistanceChannel](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iassistancechannel-0000001218157961) channel) Establishes a collaboration session with the specified ability. You need to specify the device ID, bundle name, and ability name of the peer ability through intent. Both parties in a collaboration session must be Page abilities, which belong to the same application and share the bundle name and signature. The peer ability needs to implement the [IAssistanceChannel](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iassistancechannel-0000001218157961) API. This method works only when the devices involved in the collaboration session are connected through the same WLAN. The collaboration result is sent to your application through the [IAssistanceChannel](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iassistancechannel-0000001218157961) callback. **Permissions:**[SystemPermission.DISTRIBUTED_DATASYNC](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__DISTRIBUTED_DATASYNC) **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the ability with which a collaboration session is to be established.  
channel  |  Indicates the channel used to receive the collaboration session establishment result sent using the [IAssistanceChannel](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iassistancechannel-0000001218157961) callback.  
**Since:** 7 [h2]stopAssistAbility public final int stopAssistAbility​([AbilityAgent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityagent-0000001217996497) agent) Disconnects this collaboration session. You need to pass [AbilityAgent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityagent-0000001217996497) to specify the ability from which you want to disconnect the collaboration session. After your application successfully calls [Ability#startAssistAbility](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAssistAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-ohos_aafwk_ability_assistance_IAssistanceChannel-) to establish a collaboration session, the framework calls [IAssistanceChannel#onConnected](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iassistancechannel-0000001218157961#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001218157961__onConnected-ohos_aafwk_ability_assistance_AbilityAgent-) to notify your application of the result. After the collaboration session is disconnected, the framework uses [IAssistanceChannel#onDisconnected](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iassistancechannel-0000001218157961#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001218157961__onDisconnected-int-) to notify your app. To cancel establishment of a collaboration session, call this method and pass null. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
agent  |  Indicates the ability from which this collaboration session is to be disconnected.  
**Returns:** Returns the result of disconnecting this collaboration session. Returns [AssistantCode#OK](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/assistantcode-0000001172716620#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001172716620__OK) if the disconnecting succeeds; returns an error code defined in [AssistantCode](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/assistantcode-0000001172716620) otherwise. **Since:** 7 [h2]setAdmissionTransitionObject public void setAdmissionTransitionObject​([Transition](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/transition-0000001054358733) transition) Sets the entrance transition animation type. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
transition  | IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if the ability is not initialized.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]isTerminating

public boolean isTerminating​()

Checks whether the current ability is being destroyed. 

An ability is being destroyed if you called [terminateAbility()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__terminateAbility--) on it or someone else requested to destroy it.

**Returns:**

Returns true if the current ability is being destroyed; returns false otherwise.

**Since:**

3

**See Also:**

[terminateAbility()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__terminateAbility--)

#### [h2]setIntent

public void setIntent​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent)

Sets the Intent object that can be obtained by calling [getIntent()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__getIntent--). 

This method does not copy the given Intent object, instead, it holds a reference to the given object and takes effect directly on it. This method is often used with [onNewIntent(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onNewIntent-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-).

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the Intent object to set.  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]onWindowFocusChanged

public void onWindowFocusChanged​(boolean hasFocus)

Called when this ability gains or loses window focus. 

The focus state refers to the global state, which is independent of the ability lifecycle states. Although the lifecycle state change of an ability may lead to a focus change (for example, [onStop()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStop--) may cause the ability to lose window focus), there is no particular sequence between this callback and other lifecycle callbacks such as [onBackground()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onBackground--). 

Generally, the ability in the foreground will have window focus. The ability will lose focus when another dialog box or pop-up window is displayed. The ability will also lose focus when a system-level window (such as the status bar or a system alarm) is displayed.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
hasFocus  |  Specifies whether this ability has focus.  
  
**Since:**

3

**Returns: onStoreDataWhenConfigChange

public [Object](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446) onStoreDataWhenConfigChange​()

Called to save the data to be destroyed when the system configuration is changed. This method can return any required object, including the ability instance itself. You can call [AbilityContext.getLastStoredDataWhenConfigChanged()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054358759__getLastStoredDataWhenConfigChanged--) to obtain the saved data in a new ability instance. 

**Returns:**

Returns an object of the saved data.

**Since:**

4

#### [h2]onRestoreAbilityState

public void onRestoreAbilityState​([PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) inState)

Restores data and states of an ability when it is restored by the system. This method should be implemented by a Page ability. 

This method is called if an ability was destroyed at a certain time due to resource reclaim or was unexpectedly destroyed and the [onSaveAbilityState(ohos.utils.PacMap)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onSaveAbilityState-ohos_utils_PacMap-) method was called to save its user data and states. Generally, this method is called after the [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
inState  |  Indicates the [PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) object used for storing data and states. This parameter can not be null.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onRequestPermissionsFromUserResult

public void onRequestPermissionsFromUserResult​(int requestCode, [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460)[] permissions, int[] grantResults)

Called back after permissions are requested by using [AbilityContext.requestPermissionsFromUser(java.lang.String[],int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054358759__requestPermissionsFromUser-java_lang_String:A-int-). 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
requestCode  |  Indicates the request code passed to this method from [AbilityContext.requestPermissionsFromUser(java.lang.String[],int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054358759__requestPermissionsFromUser-java_lang_String:A-int-).  
permissions  |  Indicates the list of permissions requested by using [AbilityContext.requestPermissionsFromUser(java.lang.String[],int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054358759__requestPermissionsFromUser-java_lang_String:A-int-). This parameter cannot be null.  
grantResults  |  Indicates the granting results of the corresponding permissions requested using [AbilityContext.requestPermissionsFromUser(java.lang.String[],int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054358759__requestPermissionsFromUser-java_lang_String:A-int-). The value 0 indicates that a permission is granted, and the value -1 indicates not.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]onNewDescription

public [CharSequence](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/charsequence-0000001054678442) onNewDescription​()

Called before this ability is background to generate a new description for this ability. This method is designed to return a textual description of an ability's current state to be displayed to the user.

Returns a brief description of the current user behavior.

**Since:**

3

#### [h2]onLeaveForeground

public void onLeaveForeground​()

Called when this ability is about to leave the foreground and enter the background due to a user operation, for example, when the user touches the Home key. 

**Since:**

3

#### [h2]onEventDispatch

public void onEventDispatch​()

Called every time a key, touch, or trackball event is dispatched to this ability. You can override this callback method if you want to know that the user has interacted with the device in a certain way while this ability is running. This method, together with [onLeaveForeground()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onLeaveForeground--), is designed to help abilities intelligently manage status bar notifications. Specifically, they help abilities determine when to cancel a notification. 

**Since:**

3

#### [h2]startAbility

public final void startAbility​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent) throws [IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724), [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Starts a new ability. 

A Page or Service ability uses this method to start a specific ability. The system locates the target ability from installed abilities based on the value of the intent parameter and then starts it. You can specify the ability to start using the intent parameter.

You can start a new Service ability even if the application hosting the ability is not started by adding a flag of [Intent#FLAG_START_FOREGROUND_ABILITY](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120019__FLAG_START_FOREGROUND_ABILITY).

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the ability to start.  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if the input parameter is null.  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if the ability is not initialized.  
  
**Since:**

1

[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452)

Throws this exception if this ability is not initialized or is in a state where this operation is not allowed, for example, it is being migrated.  
[UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) |  Throws this exception if this ability does not implement the IAbilityContinuation interface.  
  
**Since:**

1

#### [h2]continueAbility

public final void continueAbility​([String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) deviceId) throws [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452), [UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453)

Migrates this ability to the given device on the same distributed network. 

The ability to migrate and its ability slices must implement the [IAbilityContinuation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilitycontinuation-0000001054358657) interface. Otherwise, an exception is thrown, indicating that the ability does not support migration.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
deviceId  |  Indicates the ID of the target device where this ability will be migrated to. If this parameter is null, this method has the same effect as [continueAbility()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__continueAbility--).  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if this ability is not initialized or is in a state where this operation is not allowed, for example, it is being migrated.  
[UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) |  Throws this exception if this ability does not implement the IAbilityContinuation interface.  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]continueAbilityReversibly

public final void continueAbilityReversibly​() throws [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452), [UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453)

Migrates this ability to another device on the same distributed network in a reversible way that allows this ability to be migrated back to the local device through [reverseContinueAbility()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__reverseContinueAbility--). 

If there are multiple candidate devices, a pop-up will be displayed for users to choose the desired one. 

The ability to migrate and its ability slices must implement the [IAbilityContinuation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilitycontinuation-0000001054358657) interface. Otherwise, an exception is thrown, indicating that the ability does not support migration.

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if this ability is not initialized or is in a state where this operation is not allowed, for example, it is being migrated.  
[UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) |  Throws this exception if this ability does not implement the IAbilityContinuation interface.  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]continueAbilityReversibly

public final void continueAbilityReversibly​([String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) deviceId) throws [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452), [UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453)

Migrates this ability to the given device on the same distributed network in a reversible way that allows this ability to be migrated back to the local device through [reverseContinueAbility()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__reverseContinueAbility--). 

The ability to migrate and its ability slices must implement the [IAbilityContinuation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/iabilitycontinuation-0000001054358657) interface. Otherwise, an exception is thrown, indicating that the ability does not support migration.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
deviceId  |  Indicates the ID of the target device where this ability will be migrated to. If this parameter is null, this method has the same effect as [continueAbilityReversibly()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__continueAbilityReversibly--).  
  
**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if this ability is not initialized or is in a state where this operation is not allowed, for example, it is being migrated.  
[UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) |  Throws this exception if this ability does not implement the IAbilityContinuation interface.  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]reverseContinueAbility

public final boolean reverseContinueAbility​() throws [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452), [UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453)

Migrates this ability from another device on the same distributed network back to the local device. 

**Returns:**

Returns true if the migration request is successful; returns false otherwise.

**Throws:**

Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if this ability was not involved in a reversible migration process, or if it is in a state where this operation is not allowed, for example, it is being migrated back.  
[UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) |  Throws this exception if this ability does not implement the IAbilityContinuation interface.  
  
**Since:**

3
